  if(role==='vaela')return {title:'Bếp lửa của Vaela',intro:'Vaela cần chuẩn bị thảo dược trước khi gia đình trở về.',question:'What would you say to offer help?',choices:['I can help you prepare the herbs.','I can to helping the herbs.','I help herbs yesterday tomorrow.'],correct:0,success:'Vaela đã sắp xếp góc thảo dược.   if(a==='art-district')return {title:'Không gian triển lãm',intro:'Một nghệ sĩ muốn nghe ý kiến về cách trình bày tác phẩm.',question:'Give a clear opinion with a reason.',choices:['I prefer this design because the colours feel balanced.','Because design yes.','I preferred is tomorrow.'],correct:0,success:'Tác phẩm được giới thiệu tại khu trưng bày.',lesson:'I prefer... because... giúp nêu ý kiến và lý do.'};

  if(a==='old-oriaris')return {title:'Tư liệu hoàng gia',intro:'Một ghi chép mô tả tiếng chuông và đoàn khách đến kinh đô.',question:'Choose the correct description.',choices:['While bells ringing, guests arrives.','The guests was arriving when bells ring.','While the bells were ringing, the guests arrived.'],correct:2,success:'Tư liệu được sắp xếp trong phòng lưu trữ.',lesson:'while + was/were V-ing, sự kiện xen vào dùng quá khứ đơn.'};

  return {title:'Một ngày ở English Town',intro:'Bạn có thể giúp cư dân giải quyết một công việc nhỏ.',question:'Choose the friendly response.',choices:['I helps you tomorrow yesterday.','I would be happy to help you.','You must doing task.'],correct:1,success:'Không gian trở nên gọn gàng và ấm áp hơn.',lesson:'I would be happy to... là cách ngỏ lời lịch sự.'};
 }
 function station(sc){
  if(!sc?.inside||!sc.player)return null;
  const items=sc.inside.spec.items ||[],unit=32*sc.inside.k;
  // Follow original E interaction radius; only existing, physically drawn furniture qualifies.
  const allowed=new Set(['desk','table','shelf','counter','forge','stove','board','clock','paint','glasscase','rack','bed','altar','crystal','chair','schooldesk','glass','bookshelf']);
  let best=null,dist=Infinity;
  for(const item of items){if(!allowed.has(item.k))continue;
   const x=Yr+(item.x+item.w/2)*unit,y=Xr+(item.y+item.h/2)*unit;
   const d=Math.hypot(sc.player.x-x,sc.player.y-y);
   if(d<dist&&d<unit*2.25){best=item;dist=d;}
  }
  return best;
 }
 function ensureUI(){
  if(ui)return;
  const style=document.createElement('style');style.id='lq57-css';style.textContent=`
  #lq57-modal[hidden],#lq57-hint[hidden],#lq57-mobile[hidden]{display:none!important}
  #lq57-modal{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:40020;background:#020817cc;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:14px;font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;pointer-events:auto}
  #lq57-dialog{width:min(580px,94vw);max-height:87vh;overflow:auto;background:linear-gradient(160deg,#1b3040,#0c1826);color:#ebf7ff;border:2px solid #7fa8a0;border-radius:15px;box-shadow:0 24px 80px #000d;padding:20px;line-height:1.55}
  #lq57-dialog h2{margin:0 30px 5px 0;font-size:21px;color:#ffe2a8}#lq57-dialog p{font-size:14px;color:#c6dbe7}
  #lq57-dialog .lq57-btn{display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;margin:8px 0;color:#e6f6ff;border:1px solid #5b7a92;border-radius:9px;background:#1f3852;font-size:14px;cursor:pointer}
  #lq57-dialog .lq57-btn:hover{background:#345b77}#lq57-dialog .lq57-btn:focus-visible{outline:3px solid #ffe4a5}
  #lq57-dialog .lq57-close{float:right;background:#26394c;border:1px solid #69849e;color:white;border-radius:8px;font-size:18px;padding:4px 11px;cursor:pointer}
  #lq57-message{margin-top:9px;font-size:13px;color:#bcf2d3;min-height:20px}#lq57-kicker{font-size:11px;color:#93cfc5;letter-spacing:.11em}
  #lq57-mobile{position:fixed;right:11px;bottom:112px;z-index:45;border:1px solid #b4dcaf;border-radius:12px;padding:11px 15px;background:#1f5650;color:#fff6df;font:bold 13px system-ui;box-shadow:0 8px 25px #0009;cursor:pointer}
  #lq57-hint{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:66px;z-index:30;transform:translateX(-50%);border:1px solid #77aca7;border-radius:9px;padding:7px 12px;color:#fff0bd;background:#132b37ed;box-shadow:0 4px 15px #0008;font:12px system-ui,sans-serif;pointer-events:none;white-space:nowrap;max-width:86vw;text-overflow:ellipsis;overflow:hidden}`;
  document.head.appendChild(style);
  ui=document.createElement('div');ui.id='lq57-modal';ui.hidden=true;ui.setAttribute('role','dialog');ui.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');
  const dialog=document.createElement('div');dialog.id='lq57-dialog';ui.appendChild(dialog);document.body.appendChild(ui);
  ui.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ui)close();});
  prompt=document.createElement('div');prompt.id='lq57-hint';prompt.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(prompt);
  mobileAction=document.createElement('button');mobileAction.type='button';mobileAction.id='lq57-mobile';mobileAction.textContent='✦ Tương tác nội thất';mobileAction.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(mobileAction);
  mobileAction.onclick=()=>{const sc=window.__lifequestGetScene?.();if(sc?.inside)inspect(sc)};
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
   if(e.key==='Escape'&&!ui.hidden){e.stopPropagation();close();return;}
   if(e.code==='KeyI'&&!e.repeat&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.altKey&&!e.metaKey){
     const tag=(document.activeElement?.tagName||'').toLowerCase();
     if(['input','textarea','select'].includes(tag)||document.activeElement?.isContentEditable)return;
     const sc=window.__lifequestGetScene?.();
     if(sc?.inside&&station(sc)&&!sc.chat?.isOpen&&ui.hidden){e.preventDefault();inspect(sc);}
   }
  },true);
 }
 function close(){if(ui)ui.hidden=true;sceneNow=null;}
 function show(sc){
  ensureUI();const d=sc.inside?.spec.__lq57;if(!d)return false;
  sceneNow=sc;ui.hidden=false;const card=scenario(d),done=d.complete,content=document.getElementById('lq57-dialog');content.replaceChildren();
  const exit=document.createElement('button');exit.type='button';exit.className='lq57-close';exit.textContent='×';exit.onclick=close;content.appendChild(exit);
  const kick=document.createElement('div');kick.id='lq57-kicker';kick.textContent=`LIFEQUEST · ${d.area.toUpperCase()} · LIVING INTERIORS`;content.appendChild(kick);
  const h=document.createElement('h2');h.textContent=card.title;content.appendChild(h);
  const intro=document.createElement('p');intro.textContent=card.intro;content.appendChild(intro);
  if(done){const p=document.createElement('p');p.textContent='✓ Đã hoàn thành. '+card.success;content.appendChild(p);
   const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='lq57-btn';b.textContent='Đóng và tiếp tục khám phá';b.onclick=close;content.appendChild(b);return true;}
  const question=document.createElement('p');question.textContent=card.question;question.style.color='#fff4ce';content.appendChild(question);
  const msg=document.createElement('div');msg.id='lq57-message';msg.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
  card.choices.forEach((choice,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='lq57-btn';b.textContent=String.fromCharCode(65+i)+'. '+choice;
   b.onclick=()=>{if(i!==card.correct){msg.style.color='#ffd09d';msg.textContent='Chưa chính xác. Gợi ý: '+card.lesson;return;}
    const st=state(sc);st.completed[d.key]=true;st.interactions=(st.interactions||0)+1;st.rooms[d.key]={at:Date.now(),area:d.area,building:d.building};d.complete=true;
    sc.store.save();refresh(sc);msg.style.color='#baf7ca';msg.textContent='✓ '+card.success+' '+card.lesson;
    content.querySelectorAll('.lq57-btn').forEach(x=>{x.disabled=true;x.style.opacity='.63'});
    const cont=document.createElement('button');cont.type='button';cont.className='lq57-btn';cont.textContent='Tiếp tục khám phá căn phòng';cont.onclick=close;content.appendChild(cont);
    try{window.LQStory?.onEvent?.('inspect',{area:d.area,building:d.building,object:'lq57-restoration'});}catch(_){ }
   };content.appendChild(b);
  });content.appendChild(msg);return true;
 }
 function inspect(sc){if(!sc.inside||!station(sc))return false;return show(sc);}
 function refresh(sc){
  const inside=sc.inside;if(!inside)return;
  const k='v57_room_'+hash(inside.spec.__lq57?.key||'')+'_'+(++frameCounter);
  try{const gfx=sc.add.graphics();Dr(gfx,inside.spec);gfx.generateTexture(k,inside.spec.w*32,inside.spec.h*32);gfx.destroy();
   sc.textures.get(k).setFilter(Y.Textures.FilterMode.NEAREST);
   const prev=inside.__v57texture;inside.objs[0].setTexture(k);inside.__v57texture=k;
   if(prev&&sc.textures.exists(prev))sc.textures.remove(prev);
  }catch(e){console.warn('V57 room refresh issue:',e)}
 }
 function onEnter(sc){
  if(!sc.inside)return;ensureUI();const a=sc.inside;
  preEnter(sc,sc.area.id,a.b.name,a.spec,a.floor);
  // The first room texture was already produced after preEnter() in enterBuilding.
  if(!a.__v57layer){const layer=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(1.3);a.objs.push(layer);a.__v57layer=layer;}
  // Restore the saved room state if it was changed after the cached texture was first produced.
  if(a.spec.__lq57?.complete)refresh(sc);
 }
 const residentState=new WeakMap();
 function animateResident(sc,n,delta){
  if(!sc.inside||!n?.sprite?.visible)return;
  let st=residentState.get(n);if(!st){st={x:n.sprite.x,y:n.sprite.y,clock:0,phase:0};residentState.set(n,st)}
  st.clock+=Math.min(50,Math.max(0,delta));
  if(st.clock>8800){st.clock=0;st.phase=(st.phase+1)%3;}
  const minute=sc.store.game?.minutes||720;const open=minute>=450&&minute<=1190;
  const shift=st.phase===0?0:st.phase===1?(open?11:5):-8;
  const destX=st.x+shift*sc.inside.k;
  const safe=(!sc.blocked(destX,n.sprite.y)&&Math.abs(sc.player.x-destX)>14*sc.inside.k);
  const x=safe?destX:st.x;
  n.sprite.x+=(x-n.sprite.x)*Math.min(.06,delta/220);
 }
 let tick=0;
 function update(sc,dt){
  if(!sc?.inside){if(prompt)prompt.hidden=true;if(mobileAction)mobileAction.hidden=true;return;}
  if(!ui)ensureUI();
  // Display a compact E hint only if the interaction is actually reachable.
  tick+=Math.min(60,dt||16);if(tick>160){tick=0;
   const st=station(sc), showHint=!!st&&!sc.chat?.isOpen&&!sc.briefing?.isOpen&&ui.hidden;
   prompt.hidden=!showHint;
   const device=document.documentElement.dataset.device||'';if(mobileAction)mobileAction.hidden=!(showHint&&(device==='phone'||device==='tablet'));
   if(showHint){const d=sc.inside.spec.__lq57;prompt.textContent=`E · ${st.k} — ${d?.complete?'Xem thành quả':'Hoạt động và khám phá'}`;}
  }
  const layer=sc.inside.__v57layer;if(!layer)return;
  const d=sc.inside.spec.__lq57;const ms=sc.store.game?.minutes||720;
  layer.clear();const k=sc.inside.k, xx=Yr, yy=Xr;
  // Small, non-solid animated lighting effects; always behind the player and NPCs.
  const t=((sc.time?.now||0)/1000);
  const points=d?.style==='industrial'?[[.24,.11],[.74,.11]]:d?.style==='living'?[[.32,.12],[.68,.12]]:d?.style==='royal'?[[.19,.13],[.81,.13]]:[[.3,.14],[.7,.14]];
  const W=sc.inside.spec.w*32*k,H=sc.inside.spec.h*32*k;
  for(let i=0;i<points.length;i++){const p=points[i],x=xx+p[0]*W,y=yy+p[1]*H,a=.27+.18*Math.sin(t*1.8+i);
   layer.fillStyle(d?.style==='tech'?0x78fbf6:d?.style==='industrial'?0xffc27c:0xffdcab,a).fillCircle(x,y,3*k);
   layer.fillStyle(0xffe4a1,Math.max(.01,a*.22)).fillCircle(x,y,11*k);
  }
  if(d?.complete&&ms>=1120){const x=xx+W*.5,y=yy+H*.17;layer.fillStyle(0xe8d298,.25).fillCircle(x,y,9*k);}
 }
 function onLeave(){if(prompt)prompt.hidden=true;if(mobileAction)mobileAction.hidden=true;close();}
 function decorateExterior(sc){
  if(!sc?.area?.buildings?.length||!sc?.add||!sc?.store)return 0;
  if(Array.isArray(sc.__lq57Outdoor))for(const x of sc.__lq57Outdoor){const i=sc.areaObjs?.indexOf(x)??-1;if(i>=0)sc.areaObjs.splice(i,1);try{x.destroy();}catch(_){}}
  sc.__lq57Outdoor=[];
  const st=state(sc);let count=0;
  for(const b of sc.area.buildings){
   if(!st?.completed?.[key(sc.area.id,b.name,0)])continue;
   const x=b.door*32+16,y=(b.y+b.h)*32-45;
   const gfx=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(11);
   gfx.fillStyle(0xeed790,.25).fillCircle(x,y,14);
   gfx.fillStyle(0xffd88c,1).fillRect(x-3,y-9,6,17);
   gfx.fillStyle(0xfff4cd,1).fillRect(x-8,y-4,16,6);
   gfx.fillStyle(0x4d926b,1).fillRect(x-2,y+4,4,7);
   sc.__lq57Outdoor.push(gfx);sc.areaObjs?.push(gfx);count++;
  }
  return count;
 }
 function diagnostics(){const s=window.__lifequestGetScene?.();return {version:'57.0',currentRoom:s?.inside?.spec?.__lq57||null,hasStation:!!station(s),completed:Object.keys(s?.store?.game?.v57?.completed||{}).length,registeredStyles:Object.keys(areaStyles).length};}
 return Object.freeze({version:'57.0',preEnter,onEnter,onLeave,drawBack:art,inspect,station,update,animateResident,scenario,diagnostics,decorateExterior,refresh,close});
})();

 function classify(area,building){const n=String(building).toLowerCase();
  if(area==='wish-valley'&&/vaela|root home/.test(n))return 'vaela';
  if(area==='mining-town'&&/mine|workshop|smithy|freight/.test(n))return 'mine';
  if(area==='old-oriaris'&&/opera/.test(n))return 'opera';
  if(area==='vocalt-greedy-land'&&/chronos|clock|bellwright/.test(n))return 'clock';
  return STYLE[area]||'civic';
 }
 function preEnter(sc,area,building,spec,floor){if(!spec)return;
  const id=area+'::'+building+'::'+floor;
  const d={area,building,floor,id,kind:classify(area,building),seed:idHash(id),w:spec.w,h:spec.h};
  d.complete=!!saved(sc)?.rooms?.[id]?.completed;spec.__lq58=d;return d;
 }
 // Draw helpers: crisp integer pixel geometry, shading layers and authored compositions.
 function painter(g,s){const d=s.__lq58;if(!d)return null;const p=PAL[STYLE[d.area]]||PAL.civic;const W=s.w*32,H=s.h*32;
  const r=(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0)g.fillStyle(c,a).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h));};
  const ell=(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillEllipse(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h));
  const circ=(x,y,rad,c,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillCircle(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),rad);
  const ln=(x,y,X,Y,c,a=1,w=2)=>g.lineStyle(w,c,a).lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(X),Math.round(Y));
  const tr=(x,y,X,Y,U,V,c,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillTriangle(x,y,X,Y,U,V);
  const rectOutline=(x,y,w,h,fill,edge)=>{r(x-3,y-3,w+6,h+7,p.ink);r(x,y,w,h,fill);r(x+2,y+2,w-4,2,p.light,.45);r(x+3,y+h-5,w-6,3,edge||p.shade,.8)};
  const detail=(x,y,seed=0)=>{r(x,y,3,2,p.light,.6);r(x+6,y+3,2,2,p.accent,.7);r(x+11,y-1,2,2,p.ink,.6)};
  return {d,p,W,H,r,ell,circ,ln,tr,rectOutline,detail};
 }
 function windowScene(c,x,y,w,h,type){const {p,r,ell,circ,ln,rectOutline}=c;
  rectOutline(x,y,w,h,p.shade,p.wood);
  r(x+5,y+5,w-10,h-10,type==='maorai'?0x447965:type==='mine'?0x355360:type==='opera'?0x334465:type==='clock'?0x86614d:0x70a2a9);
  const yy=y+h*.67;
  if(type==='maorai'){ell(x+w*.5,yy,w*.88,h*.6,0x244e40);ell(x+w*.28,yy-7,31,30,0x6b9a62);ell(x+w*.74,yy-10,42,36,0x80a56b);circ(x+w*.66,y+14,6,0xf5db96,.9);}
  else if(type==='opera'){r(x+5,yy,w-10,3,0xbd95a7);for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const xx=x+9+i*(w-15)/6;r(xx,yy-19,3,19,0x8b7087);}}
  else if(type==='mine'){r(x+6,yy-10,w-12,13,0x243d4b);for(let j=0;j<5;j++){r(x+9+j*(w-16)/5,yy-7,8,5,0x67b4ad,.6)}}
  else{r(x+5,yy-8,w-10,9,p.wood,.5);for(let j=0;j<5;j++)r(x+7+j*(w-14)/5,yy-12,4,12,p.ink,.45)}
  ln(x+w*.5,y+5,x+w*.5,y+h-5,p.wood,1,4);r(x+6,y+5,w-12,2,p.light,.45);
 }
 function cog(c,x,y,rad,phase=0){const {p,r,circ}=c;circ(x,y,rad+4,p.ink);circ(x,y,rad,p.light);circ(x,y,rad-4,p.wood);circ(x,y,rad-9,p.ink);
  for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const t=i*Math.PI/6+phase;r(x+Math.cos(t)*(rad+1)-3,y+Math.sin(t)*(rad+1)-3,6,6,p.light)}circ(x,y,4,p.accent);}
 function vegetation(c,x,y,size=1){const {p,r,ell,ln}=c;
  ln(x,y+23*size,x-6*size,y-4*size,p.wood,1,6*size);ln(x,y+10*size,x+9*size,y-12*size,p.wood,1,4*size);
  for(const [dx,dy,w,h,col] of [[-16,-8,28,24,0x477752],[3,-13,29,25,0x7fa26b],[-4,-23,24,23,0x609665],[11,-2,19,16,0x86b277]])ell(x+dx*size,y+dy*size,w*size,h*size,col);
  r(x-7*size,y-19*size,4*size,3*size,p.light,.56);
 }
 function showcase(c,type){const {d,p,W,H,r,ell,ln,tr,circ,rectOutline}=c;const cx=W/2;
  const b=s=>Math.round(s);const complete=!!d.complete;
  // Main focal zone at wall. A wide, detailed vignette gives every room its own silhouette.
  if(type==='vaela'||type==='maorai'){
    // Branch arch: rings, exposed organic rafters, alcoves, hanging plants and a central hearth.

      for(let j=0;j<3;j++){r(cx-86+i*64,31+j*6,27-(j%2)*8,2,j===0?p.accent:p.light,.65)}}
    for(let i=-1;i<=1;i+=2){cog(c,cx+i*133,58,23);r(cx+i*133-15,78,30,9,p.wood)}
    for(let x of [17,W-30]){r(x,89,14,H-166,p.ink,.78);r(x+4,97,3,H-186,p.accent,.55);for(let y=94;y<H-75;y+=36)r(x-6,y,26,5,p.light,.4)}
    // Survey rails are INLAID floor marks, not fake solid rails.

    for(const dx of [-48,48]){r(cx+dx-4,83,5,H-145,0xa0bbc1,.39);r(cx+dx+6,83,5,H-145,0xa0bbc1,.39)}

 function drawBack(g,s){if(!s.__lq58)return;const c=painter(g,s);if(!c)return;
  const {d,p,W,H,r,ln,ell}=c;let kind=d.kind;
  // Strong coherent floor mass and edge-shadow to separate near/far planes.
  r(7,71,W-14,H-82,p.base,.62);r(12,75,W-24,7,p.ink,.46);
  for(let y=91;y<H-23;y+=32){for(let x=14;x<W-10;x+=32){
    const alternate=(((x/32|0)+(y/32|0))&1);r(x,y,30,30,alternate?p.wood:p.shade,.13);
    r(x+2,y+2,25,1,p.light,.09);r(x+4,y+27,21,2,p.ink,.14);
  }}
  // Layout silhouette as floor inlay; 2 side wings and a central path create depth without blocking.
  r(22,85,Math.max(35,W*.22),H-139,p.ink,.13);r(W-22-Math.max(35,W*.22),85,Math.max(35,W*.22),H-139,p.ink,.13);
  r(W*.37,93,W*.26,H-159,p.light,.06);
  showcase(c,kind);
  for(let x=15;x<W-15;x+=41){r(x,H-23,31,3,p.light,.19);r(x+6,H-18,16,1,p.ink,.3)}
 }
 function drawItem(g,it,s){
  if(!s?.__lq58||!it||!Number.isFinite(it.x)||!Number.isFinite(it.y))return;
  const d=s.__lq58,p=PAL[STYLE[d.area]]||PAL.civic,k=it.k,kind=d.kind;
  const x=it.x*32,y=it.y*32,w=it.w*32,h=it.h*32;
  const r=(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0)g.fillStyle(c,a).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h))};
  const el=(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillEllipse(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),w,h);
  const ln=(x,y,X,Y,c,a=1,sw=2)=>g.lineStyle(sw,c,a).lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(X),Math.round(Y));
  const shadow=()=>el(x+w*.5,y+h*.83,w*.84,Math.min(h*.34,17),p.ink,.3);

 function close(){if(overlay)overlay.hidden=true;opened=null;}
 function nearestItem(sc){if(!sc?.inside||!sc.player)return null;
  const spec=sc.inside.spec,unit=32*sc.inside.k,good=new Set(['desk','table','counter','shelf','board','clock','forge','anvil','crystal','glasscase','rack','stove','paint','altar','computer','terminal','bed','cabinet','bookshelf','workbench','minecart']);
  let closest=null,best=Infinity;for(const it of spec.items||[]){if(!good.has(it.k))continue;
   const x=Yr+(it.x+it.w*.5)*unit,y=Xr+(it.y+it.h*.5)*unit;const dist=Math.hypot(sc.player.x-x,sc.player.y-y);
   if(dist<best&&dist<unit*2.6){closest=it;best=dist;}}
  return closest;
 }
 function nearby(sc){if(!sc?.inside)return false;return !!nearestItem(sc)}
 function doSave(sc){try{sc.store?.save?.()}catch(e){console.warn('[LQ58] save:',e)}}
 function advance(sc,d,step){const st=saved(sc);if(!st)return;const rec=roomRecord(sc,d);
  if(rec.step!==step)return;
  rec.step++;rec.lastAt=Date.now();rec.visited=Math.max(1,rec.visited||0);
  if(rec.step>=scenario(d).steps.length){rec.completed=true;st.chronicle.unshift({room:d.id,area:d.area,building:d.building,day:sc.store.game?.day||1});st.chronicle=st.chronicle.slice(0,40);
   st.choices[d.id]='restored';}
  doSave(sc);
  // Redraw the architecture once after every successful stage, including completion.
  refreshRoom(sc);
  if(rec.completed){try{window.LQStory?.onEvent?.('inspect',{area:d.area,building:d.building,object:'lq58-restored'});}catch(_){}}
 }
 function refreshRoom(sc){if(!sc?.inside?.spec?.__lq58)return;const d=sc.inside.spec.__lq58;d.complete=!!roomRecord(sc,d).completed;
  try{LQ57.refresh(sc);}catch(e){console.warn('[LQ58] room repaint:',e)}
 }
 function renderPuzzle(sc,d,question,step){
   const box=overlay.querySelector('#lq58-puzzle');if(!box)return;
   const rec=roomRecord(sc,d),pz=question.puzzle;
   if(!rec.puzzle||rec.puzzle.step!==step){rec.puzzle={step,chosen:[],gears:[0,0,0]};doSave(sc)}
   const progress=rec.puzzle;
   const finish=()=>{
     advance(sc,d,step);rec.puzzle=null;doSave(sc);
     box.innerHTML='<p style="color:#9cf2cb;font-weight:700">✓ '+safeText(question.feedback)+'</p>';
     const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='lq58-option';b.textContent=rec.completed?'✓ Xem căn phòng đã hoàn thiện':'→ Chuyển sang hoạt động tiếp theo';b.onclick=()=>render(sc);box.appendChild(b);
   };
   if(pz.kind==='sequence'){
     const order=pz.order;
     const renderStage=()=>{
       const chosen=progress.chosen||[];
       box.innerHTML='<div class="lq58-puzzle-stage"><div class="lq58-eyebrow">THỨ TỰ THAO TÁC</div><div class="lq58-slot-row"></div><div class="lq58-token-row"></div><p class="lq58-muted" id="lq58-puzzle-message">Chọn đúng thao tác tiếp theo để hoàn thiện công việc.</p></div>';
       const slots=box.querySelector('.lq58-slot-row');for(let i=0;i<order.length;i++){const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='lq58-slot'+(chosen[i]!==undefined?' filled':'');el.textContent=chosen[i]!==undefined?pz.items[chosen[i]]:`Bước ${i+1}`;slots.appendChild(el)}
       const row=box.querySelector('.lq58-token-row');pz.items.forEach((name,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='lq58-token';b.textContent=name;b.disabled=chosen.includes(i);b.onclick=()=>{
         if(!opened||opened.sc!==sc)return;
         if(i!==order[chosen.length]){box.querySelector('#lq58-puzzle-message').textContent='Chưa đúng thứ tự. Hãy đối chiếu yêu cầu và thử thao tác khác.';return;}
         chosen.push(i);doSave(sc);if(chosen.length===order.length){finish();return;}renderStage();
       };row.appendChild(b)});
     };renderStage();return;
   }
   if(pz.kind==='gears'){
     const renderGears=()=>{
       box.innerHTML='<div class="lq58-puzzle-stage"><div class="lq58-eyebrow">CLOCKWORK ALIGNMENT · TƯƠNG TÁC CƠ KHÍ</div><div class="lq58-gears"></div><p class="lq58-muted">Xoay các bánh răng đến đúng dấu khắc, rồi bộ máy sẽ sáng lên.</p></div>';
       const row=box.querySelector('.lq58-gears');pz.targets.forEach((target,i)=>{
         const n=progress.gears[i];const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='lq58-gear';b.setAttribute('aria-label','Quay bánh răng '+(i+1));
         b.innerHTML=`<b style="transform:rotate(${n*60}deg)">⚙</b><small>Bánh răng ${i+1}</small><strong>${n===target?'✓ Đã căn chỉnh':`${n*60}° / mục tiêu ${target*60}°`}</strong>`;
         b.onclick=()=>{if(!opened||opened.sc!==sc)return;progress.gears[i]=(progress.gears[i]+1)%6;doSave(sc);
           if(progress.gears.every((x,j)=>x===pz.targets[j])){finish();return}renderGears();};row.appendChild(b)});
     };renderGears();return;
   }
 }
 function render(sc){ensureUI();if(!sc?.inside)return;const d=sc.inside.spec.__lq58;if(!d)return;
  const card=scenario(d),r=roomRecord(sc,d),count=card.steps.length;
  const step=Math.min(r.step,count),done=step>=count;
  opened={sc,d};overlay.hidden=false;
  const h=`<div class="lq58-card"><div class="lq58-head"><div><div class="lq58-eyebrow">LIFEQUEST AI V58 · LIVING INTERIORS</div><h2>${card.icon} ${safeText(card.name)}</h2><div class="lq58-muted">${safeText(card.subtitle)} · ${safeText(card.station)}</div></div><button class="lq58-x" id="lq58-close" aria-label="Đóng">✕</button></div>
  <div class="lq58-progress"><i style="width:${Math.round(step/count*100)}%"></i></div>
  <div class="lq58-stat"><span>Tiến độ hoạt động</span><strong>${step}/${count}</strong></div>
  ${done?`<h3>✓ Đã hoàn thành</h3><p>Căn phòng và một số chi tiết kiến trúc thay đổi theo tiến độ. Những lựa chọn của bạn được lưu vào nhật ký.</p><button class="lq58-option" id="lq58-journal">Xem ký ức của thế giới</button>`:
   `<div class="lq58-eyebrow">CHẶNG ${step+1} · ${safeText(card.steps[step].title)}</div><p>${safeText(card.steps[step].desc)}</p><h3>${safeText(card.steps[step].question)}</h3>${card.steps[step].puzzle?'<div id="lq58-puzzle"></div>':`<div id="lq58-options">${card.steps[step].options.map((a,i)=>`<button class="lq58-option" data-answer="${i}">${String.fromCharCode(65+i)}. ${safeText(a)}</button>`).join('')}</div><p id="lq58-feedback" class="lq58-muted" aria-live="polite"></p>`}` }
  <button class="lq58-small" id="lq58-leave">Quay lại thế giới</button></div>`;
  overlay.innerHTML=h;
  overlay.querySelector('#lq58-close').onclick=close;overlay.querySelector('#lq58-leave').onclick=close;
  if(done){overlay.querySelector('#lq58-journal').onclick=()=>showChronicle(sc);return;}
  const question=card.steps[step];if(question.puzzle){renderPuzzle(sc,d,question,step);return;}overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-answer]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{
   if(!opened||opened.sc!==sc)return;const i=Number(btn.dataset.answer);const msg=overlay.querySelector('#lq58-feedback');
   if(i!==question.answer){msg.textContent='Thử lại. Gợi ý: '+question.feedback;msg.style.color='#ffcc9e';return;}
   msg.textContent='✓ '+question.feedback;msg.style.color='#aff4d8';advance(sc,d,step);
   overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-answer]').forEach(x=>x.disabled=true);

  });

 function inspect(sc){if(!sc?.inside||!nearby(sc))return false;
  if(sc.chat?.isOpen||sc.briefing?.isOpen||sc.switching)return false;
  const d=sc.inside.spec.__lq58;if(!d)return false;render(sc);return true;
 }
 function onEnter(sc){if(!sc?.inside)return;ensureUI();const d=sc.inside.spec.__lq58;if(!d)return;
  const rec=roomRecord(sc,d);rec.visited=(rec.visited||0)+1;d.complete=!!rec.completed;doSave(sc);
  const a=sc.inside;

 }
 function onLeave(){close();if(hint)hint.hidden=true;if(touch)touch.hidden=true;}
 function resident(sc,n,delta){if(!sc?.inside||!n?.sprite?.visible)return;
  let st=walkers.get(n);if(!st||st.room!==sc.inside.b.name){st={room:sc.inside.b.name,home:[n.sprite.x,n.sprite.y],target:null,path:[],tick:0,at:0,index:0};walkers.set(n,st)}
  st.tick+=clamp(delta,0,100);
  const unit=32*sc.inside.k,minute=sc.store.game?.minutes??720;const duringWork=minute>=480&&minute<1080;
  if(st.tick>6000+(idHash(n.def?.id||'npc')%3000)){
    st.tick=0;st.index++;
    const hx=st.home[0],hy=st.home[1],offsets=duringWork?[[0,0],[unit,0],[0,unit],[-unit,0],[0,-unit]]:[[0,0],[-unit,0],[0,-unit]];
    const pair=offsets[st.index%offsets.length];const tx=hx+pair[0],ty=hy+pair[1];
    if(!sc.blocked(tx,ty))st.path=indoorRoute(sc,[n.sprite.x,n.sprite.y],[tx,ty]);
  }
  if(!st.path.length)return;
  const [x,y]=st.path[0],dx=x-n.sprite.x,dy=y-n.sprite.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
  if(dist<2){st.path.shift();return}
  const speed=Math.min(dist,Math.max(.3,delta*.018));const nextX=n.sprite.x+dx/dist*speed,nextY=n.sprite.y+dy/dist*speed;
  if(sc.blocked(nextX,nextY)||Math.hypot(nextX-sc.player.x,nextY-sc.player.y)<unit*.32){st.path=[];return}
  n.sprite.setPosition(nextX,nextY);
 }
 const walkers=new WeakMap();
 function indoorRoute(sc,start,goal){ // Local BFS of walkable tile centres, no diagonal cuts through solid furniture.
  const unit=32*sc.inside.k,spec=sc.inside.spec;
  const toGrid=p=>[clamp(Math.round((p[0]-Yr)/unit-.5),1,spec.w-2),clamp(Math.round((p[1]-Xr)/unit-.5),2,spec.h-2)];
  const from=toGrid(start),dest=toGrid(goal),encode=(x,y)=>y*spec.w+x;
  const q=[[...from]],seen=new Set([encode(...from)]),prev=new Map();let j=0,stop=encode(...dest);
  while(j<q.length&&q.length<250){const [x,y]=q[j++],code=encode(x,y);if(code===stop)break;
    for(const [dx,dy] of [[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]]){const xx=x+dx,yy=y+dy;if(xx<1||xx>=spec.w-1||yy<2||yy>=spec.h-2)continue;
      const k=encode(xx,yy);if(seen.has(k))continue;const wx=Yr+(xx+.5)*unit,wy=Xr+(yy+.5)*unit;
      if(sc.blocked(wx,wy))continue;seen.add(k);prev.set(k,code);q.push([xx,yy]);}}
  if(!seen.has(stop))return [];
  const out=[];let k=stop;while(k!==encode(...from)&&out.length<18){let x=k%spec.w,y=Math.floor(k/spec.w);out.unshift([Yr+(x+.5)*unit,Xr+(y+.5)*unit]);k=prev.get(k);if(k===undefined)return []}return out;
 }
 function update(sc,delta){if(!sc||!sc.store)return;ensureUI();clock+=clamp(delta||16,0,60);
  if(!sc.inside){if(hint)hint.hidden=true;if(touch)touch.hidden=true;if(clock>1100){clock=0;worldEvent(sc)}return;}
  if(clock<95)return;clock=0;
  const room=sc.inside,d=room.spec.__lq58;if(!d)return;
  const show=nearby(sc)&&overlay.hidden&&!sc.chat?.isOpen&&!sc.briefing?.isOpen;
  if(hint){hint.hidden=!show;if(show)hint.textContent='U hoặc E · Hoạt động '+scenario(d).station}
  if(touch){const device=document.documentElement.dataset.device||'';touch.hidden=!show||!['phone','tablet'].includes(device)}
  if(overlay.hidden&&room.__lq58light){const g=room.__lq58light;g.clear();
    const minute=sc.store.game?.minutes||720,t=(sc.time?.now||0)/1000,day=minute>=450&&minute<=1080;
    const k=room.k,W=room.spec.w*32*k,xx=Yr+W/2,yy=Xr+52*k;
    let bright=day?.12:.34;const motion=!!saved(sc)?.settings?.reducedMotion;
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){let x=xx+(i-1)*Math.min(132*k,W*.26),alpha=bright+(motion?0:.055*Math.sin(t*1.9+i));
      const col=d.kind==='mine'?0x86ffe2:d.kind==='clock'?0xffd793:d.kind==='opera'?0xffc4a1:0xffe4b2;
      g.fillStyle(col,alpha*.18).fillCircle(x,yy,37*k);
      g.fillStyle(col,alpha*.4).fillCircle(x,yy-6*k,9*k);
    }
    if(roomRecord(sc,d).completed){g.fillStyle(0x91f5cb,.48).fillCircle(xx,yy+28*k,4*k)}
   }
 }
 function worldEvent(sc){if(!sc?.area||sc.inside||sc.switching)return;
  const a=sc.area.id,pack=specialEvents[a];if(!pack)return;const g=sc.store.game||{},minute=g.minutes||720;
  const stage=minute>=1080&&minute<1260?'evening':minute>=480&&minute<600?'morning':null;if(!stage)return;
  const key=[g.day||1,a,stage].join(':');if(noticeAt===key)return;noticeAt=key;
  const data=saved(sc);if(data?.events?.[key])return;
  if(data){data.events[key]=true;const keys=Object.keys(data.events);if(keys.length>70){for(const k of keys.slice(0,keys.length-65))delete data.events[k]}doSave(sc)}
  const msg=document.getElementById('lq58-toast')||(()=>{let div=document.createElement('div');div.id='lq58-toast';div.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(div);return div})();
  msg.textContent='✦ '+pack[0]+' · '+pack[1];msg.hidden=false;setTimeout(()=>{msg.hidden=true},3600);
 }
 function diagnostics(){const sc=scene();return {version:'58.0',ready:!!sc,inside:sc?.inside?.b?.name||null,kind:sc?.inside?.spec?.__lq58?.kind||null,progress:sc?.inside?roomRecord(sc,sc.inside.spec.__lq58):null,chronicle:saved(sc)?.chronicle?.length||0}};
 return Object.freeze({version:'58.0',preEnter,drawBack,drawFront,drawItem,onEnter,onLeave,inspect,nearby,update,resident,indoorRoute,showChronicle,diagnostics,scenario,refreshRoom,test:{classify,painter,scenario,indoorRoute}});
})();

 function outdoor(sc,dt){
   if(!sc?.area?.id||!sc.player||!sc.add?.graphics)return;
   if(!ambienceGr){ambienceGr=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(1.12)}
   const g=ambienceGr;g.clear();const area=sc.area.id,px=sc.player.x,py=sc.player.y;
   const kind=THEMES[area]||'civic';const motion=quality==='high'&&!sc.store?.game?.v58?.settings?.reducedMotion;
   const t=motion?(sc.time?.now||0)*.001:0;
   const h0=hash(area),unit=84;
   // World-anchored ambient marks in a fixed radius. No world geometry mutation.
   const minx=Math.floor((px-240)/unit),maxx=Math.ceil((px+240)/unit),miny=Math.floor((py-180)/unit),maxy=Math.ceil((py+180)/unit);
   let count=0;
   for(let gx=minx;gx<=maxx&&count<MAX_EFFECTS;gx++)for(let gy=miny;gy<=maxy&&count<MAX_EFFECTS;gy++){
      const h=hash(area+':'+gx+':'+gy);if(h%4!==0)continue;
      const xx=gx*unit+10+(h>>>5)%61,yy=gy*unit+15+(h>>>12)%54;
      const col=kind==='organic'?0xc9eb91:kind==='industrial'?0xe9ab70:kind==='tech'?0x8af6e6:kind==='royal'?0xffdeb4:kind==='atelier'?0xd6b5ee:kind==='bazaar'?0xffd4a1:0xe7e3b8;
      const shimmer=motion?.33+.18*Math.sin(t*1.7+(h%27)):.3;
      if(kind==='industrial'){g.fillStyle(col,shimmer).fillRect(xx,yy,3,2);g.fillStyle(0xffd18a,shimmer*.42).fillRect(xx+4,yy-3,2,2)}
      else if(kind==='organic'||kind==='woodland'){g.fillStyle(col,shimmer).fillCircle(xx,yy+(motion?Math.sin(t+h)*3:0),2.1);g.fillStyle(0x92b887,shimmer*.45).fillCircle(xx+5,yy+3,1.2)}
      else if(kind==='atelier'){g.fillStyle(col,shimmer).fillRect(xx,yy,4,2)}
      else if(kind==='tech'){g.lineStyle(1,col,shimmer).strokeCircle(xx,yy,3);g.fillStyle(col,shimmer).fillRect(xx-1,yy-1,2,2)}
      else {g.fillStyle(col,shimmer*.68).fillRect(xx,yy,2,2)}
      count++;
   }
 }

 function drawFront(g,s){if(!s?.__lq58)return;const c=context(g,s);if(!c)return;const {W,H,box,light,ink,wood,z}=c;
  box(4,84,W-8,3,ink,.63);box(15,87,W-30,2,light,.33);for(let x=15;x<W-16;x+=58){box(x,86,21,3,wood,.76);box(x+6,88,8,1,light,.48)}
  // Exact center threshold (visual decal, no collision logic).
  box(W/2-23,H-41,46,4,wood,.72);box(W/2-15,H-38,30,2,light,.53);
  if(z==='organic'){for(let x of [19,W-20]){box(x,90,7,H-138,wood,.59);box(x+2,92,2,H-144,light,.23)}}

 }
 function activeScene(){return window.__lifequestGetScene?.()}
 function attachIndoor(sc){if(!sc?.inside||!sc.add?.graphics)return;let id=sc.inside.b?.name+'|'+sc.area?.id;
  if(roomStamp===id&&animGr)return;roomStamp=id;active=sc;
  if(animGr){animGr.destroy();animGr=null}
  const g=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(1.38);sc.inside.objs.push(g);animGr=g;
 }
 function effects(sc,dt){elapsed+=clamp(dt||16,0,70);if(elapsed<70)return;elapsed=0;
  if(!sc?.inside){if(animGr)animGr.clear();roomStamp='';outdoor(sc,dt);return}
  if(ambienceGr)ambienceGr.clear();
  attachIndoor(sc);if(!animGr)return;

  const reduced=!!sc.store?.game?.v58?.settings?.reducedMotion||quality==='low';

  }

 }

 function pulse(sc){const aid=areaId(sc),meta=MAPS[aid]||TRAILS[aid];if(!meta)return null;const t=clock(sc),slot=Math.floor(t.minutes/180),eventDay=t.day%4===0;
  const eventName=eventDay&&slot>=3&&slot<6?meta.event:(slot<3?'Nghỉ ngơi':slot<5?'Sinh hoạt buổi sáng':slot<7?'Hoạt động cộng đồng':'Buổi tối yên tĩnh');

 function relation(sc,npc){const d=data(sc),id=uid(areaId(sc),npc.def?.id||npc.id||'visitor');if(!d.people[id])d.people[id]={trust:0,topics:[],lastDay:0,chats:0};return{id,info:d.people[id]}}
 function nearestNPC(sc,radius=85){if(!valid(sc)||!sc.npcs)return null;const np=sc.npcs.filter(n=>n?.sprite?.visible&&(!sc.inside||!!n.inside));let best=null,dist=radius;
  for(const n of np){const dis=Math.hypot(sc.player.x-n.sprite.x,sc.player.y-n.sprite.y);if(dis<dist){best=n;dist=dis}}return best
 }

 function mission(sc){const cfg=MAPS[areaId(sc)];if(!cfg)return null;const r=region(sc);return{cfg,r}}
 function missionAdvance(sc,type){const m=mission(sc);if(!m||m.r.complete)return false;
  if(type==='inspect'&&m.r.progress===0){m.r.progress=1;record('quest',`${m.cfg.name}: ${m.cfg.steps[0]}`,{},sc);return true}
  if(type==='assemble'&&m.r.progress===1){m.r.progress=2;record('quest',`${m.cfg.name}: ${m.cfg.steps[1]}`,{},sc);return true}
  if(type==='speak'&&m.r.progress===2){m.r.progress=3;m.r.complete=true;data(sc).artifacts['regional-'+areaId(sc)]={title:m.cfg.artifact,desc:'Kỷ vật của '+m.cfg.name};record('world-change',`${m.cfg.name}: công trình đã được cải thiện`,{},sc);publish('region-restored',{area:areaId(sc)},sc);refreshScene(sc);return true}

 function hasField(sc){return valid(sc)&&!sc.chat?.isOpen&&!sc.briefing?.isOpen&&!sc.switching}
 function nearbyBuilding(sc){const a=sc?.area;if(!a?.buildings?.length)return null;let near=null,dist=115;
  for(const b of a.buildings){const x=(Number(b.door)+.5)*32,y=(Number(b.y)+Number(b.h)+.7)*32;
   const d=Math.hypot(sc.player.x-x,sc.player.y-y);if(d<dist){dist=d;near=b}}return near
 }
 function currentObject(sc){if(!hasField(sc))return null;

  const b=sc.inside.b?.name||'',key=roomId(sc),matches=evidenceFor(a,b),cfg=MAPS[a];
  if(a==='english-town'&&b==='Library')return{kind:'museum',title:'Bảo tàng ký ức',building:b,aid:a};
  if(matches.length)return{kind:'echo',title:matches[0].title,building:b,aid:a,echo:matches[0]};
  if(cfg&&b===cfg.building)return{kind:'mission',title:cfg.artifact,building:b,aid:a};
  const n=nearestNPC(sc,76);if(n)return{kind:'npc',title:n.def?.name||'NPC',npc:n,aid:a};return null
 }
 // Candidate walkable interior tile near the center and the selected functional station.
 function interiorAnchor(sc){if(!sc?.inside)return null;
  const room=sc.inside,s=room.spec,k=room.k||1,W=s.w,H=s.h,u=TILE*k;
  const item=s.items?.find(it=>/clock|shelf|desk|table|counter|plant|paint|board|stove|vault|machine|pigeon/i.test(it.k))||null;
  const objective=item?[item.x+(item.w||1)/2,item.y+(item.h||1)/2+1.6]:[W/2,H/2];
  const toPos=(x,y)=>({x:Yr+(x+.5)*u,y:Xr+(y+.5)*u,tileX:x,tileY:y});
  const allowed=(x,y)=>x>=1&&y>=3&&x<W-1&&y<H-2&&!sc.blocked?.(Yr+(x+.5)*u,Xr+(y+.5)*u);
  let sx=clamp(Math.floor((sc.player.x-Yr)/u),1,W-2),sy=clamp(Math.floor((sc.player.y-Xr)/u),3,H-3);
  if(!allowed(sx,sy)){let found=false;for(let r=1;r<4&&!found;r++)for(let dy=-r;dy<=r&&!found;dy++)for(let dx=-r;dx<=r;dx++){if(allowed(sx+dx,sy+dy)){sx+=dx;sy+=dy;found=true;break}}if(!found)return null}
  const q=[[sx,sy]],seen=new Set([sy*W+sx]);let j=0,best=toPos(sx,sy),min=Infinity;
  while(j<q.length&&j<W*H){const [x,y]=q[j++];
    const dist=(x-objective[0])**2+(y-objective[1])**2+(y===H-3?3:0);
    if(dist<min){min=dist;best=toPos(x,y)}
    for(const [dx,dy] of [[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]]){const xx=x+dx,yy=y+dy,key=yy*W+xx;if(seen.has(key)||!allowed(xx,yy))continue;seen.add(key);q.push([xx,yy])}
  }
  return best
 }

 function cleanupGraphics(){for(const x of markers){try{x.destroy()}catch{}}markers=[];canvasLayer=null;latest=null}

 }

 function guide(sc){const n=nearestNPC(sc,100);if(!n)return{ok:false,message:'Hãy đến gần một NPC trước khi chọn người đồng hành.'};
  const key=n.def?.id||'guide';if(followerSource===uid(areaId(sc),key)){clearFollower();return{ok:true,message:'Đã kết thúc hành trình đồng hành.'}}
  clearFollower();try{const frame=n.sprite.frame?.name,clone=sc.add.sprite(n.sprite.x,n.sprite.y,n.sprite.texture.key,frame).setDepth(14).setScale(1);clone.setAlpha(.85);

 }
 function clearFollower(){try{follower?.sprite?.destroy()}catch{}follower=null;followerSource='';followTarget=null}
 // Bounded local BFS prevents companions from cutting through walls or crossing locked gates.
 function followerRoute(sc,start,goal){const W=sc.area.w,H=sc.area.h,T=TILE;
  const sx=clamp(Math.floor(start.x/T),1,W-2),sy=clamp(Math.floor(start.y/T),1,H-2);
  const gx=clamp(Math.floor(goal.x/T),1,W-2),gy=clamp(Math.floor(goal.y/T),1,H-2);
  const allowed=(x,y)=>x>=1&&y>=1&&x<W-1&&y<H-1&&Math.abs(x-sx)<=22&&Math.abs(y-sy)<=22&&!sc.blocked?.((x+.5)*T,(y+.5)*T)&&!sc.area.warpAt?.(x,y);
  if(!allowed(sx,sy))return[];
  const id=(x,y)=>y*W+x,q=[[sx,sy]],visited=new Set([id(sx,sy)]),parents=new Map();let k=0,stop=null,best=id(sx,sy),mind=Infinity;
  while(k<q.length&&k<1450){const [x,y]=q[k++],code=id(x,y),dis=Math.abs(x-gx)+Math.abs(y-gy);if(dis<mind){mind=dis;best=code}if(dis<=2){stop=code;break}
   for(const [dx,dy] of [[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]]){const xx=x+dx,yy=y+dy,key=id(xx,yy);if(visited.has(key)||!allowed(xx,yy))continue;visited.add(key);parents.set(key,code);q.push([xx,yy])}
  }
  const target=stop??best,origin=id(sx,sy),pts=[];let node=target;
  while(node!==origin&&parents.has(node)&&pts.length<100){pts.push({x:(node%W+.5)*T,y:(Math.floor(node/W)+.5)*T});node=parents.get(node)}
  pts.reverse();return pts;
 }
 function walkFollower(sc,dt){if(!follower)return;if(sc!==follower.sc||areaId(sc)!==follower.area||sc.inside){clearFollower();return}
  const p=sc.player,sprite=follower.sprite,x=sprite.x,y=sprite.y,dist=Math.hypot(p.x-x,p.y-y);if(dist<58){follower.path=[];return}
  followerAcc+=clamp(dt,0,100);
  if(followerAcc>=1050||!follower.path?.length){followerAcc=0;follower.path=followerRoute(sc,{x,y},{x:p.x,y:p.y})}
  const waypoint=follower.path?.[0];if(!waypoint)return;
  const dx=waypoint.x-x,dy=waypoint.y-y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(d<6){follower.path.shift();return}
  const speed=Math.min(d,76*clamp(dt,0,100)/1000),nx=x+dx/d*speed,ny=y+dy/d*speed;
  if(!sc.blocked?.(nx,ny)&&!sc.area.warpAt?.(Math.floor(nx/TILE),Math.floor(ny/TILE)))sprite.setPosition(nx,ny);else follower.path=[];
 }
 let lastUpdate=0;
 function update(sc,dt){if(!valid(sc))return;const dtime=clamp(Number(dt)||16,0,100);
  walkFollower(sc,dtime);timeAccumulator+=dtime;if(timeAccumulator<450)return;timeAccumulator=0;
  if(areaId(sc)!==lastArea)onArea(sc);if(!!sc.inside&&roomId(sc)!==lastRoom)onEnter(sc);

 }

   if(m){const rr=m.r,steps=m.cfg.steps;html+=block('Nhiệm vụ: '+esc(m.cfg.name),`<p>${rr.complete?'<span class="done">✓ Đã phục hồi công trình</span>':`Tiến độ ${rr.progress}/3 · ${esc(steps[Math.min(2,rr.progress)])}`}</p><div class="actions"><button data-v60="mission">Bắt đầu / Tiếp tục nhiệm vụ</button></div>`)}
   html+='<div class="card">'+Object.entries(MAPS).map(([id,c])=>`<p>${d.regions[id]?.complete?'✓':'○'} ${esc(c.name)} — ${Math.min(3,d.regions[id]?.progress||0)}/3</p>`).join('')+'</div>';
  }else if(tab==='echo'){
   html='<h3>Time Echo — Ký ức xuyên thời gian</h3><p>Đến đúng công trình ở hai thời kỳ hoặc hai vùng, tìm vật chứng và đối chiếu. Các manh mối đã phát hiện sẽ được lưu lại.</p>';
   for(const e of ECHO){const st=echoState(sc,e);html+=block(esc(e.title),`<p>${esc(e.description)}</p><p>${st.source?'✓':'○'} ${esc(MAPS[e.source[0]].name)} · ${esc(e.source[1])}</p><p>${st.target?'✓':'○'} ${esc(MAPS[e.target[0]].name)} · ${esc(e.target[1])}</p>${st.complete?'<p class="done">✓ Ký ức hoàn thiện</p>':''}`)}
   if(sc?.inside){const match=evidenceFor(aid,sc.inside.b.name);if(match.length)html+='<div class="actions"><button data-v60="echo:inspect">🔎 Kiểm tra vật chứng trong phòng</button></div>'}
  }else if(tab==='people'){
   html='<h3>NPC Social Graph</h3><p>Tìm hiểu cư dân và xây dựng mối quan hệ qua lựa chọn. Các nhân vật có lịch hoạt động khác nhau theo thời gian và hoàn thành nhiệm vụ.</p>';
   if(n){const rel=relation(sc,n);html+=block('Nhân vật gần bạn: '+esc(n.def?.name||n.def?.id),`<p>Tin tưởng ${rel.info.trust}/5 · Đã trò chuyện ${rel.info.chats} lần</p><div class="actions"><button data-v60="npc:help">Đề nghị hỗ trợ</button><button data-v60="npc:story">Hỏi về câu chuyện</button><button data-v60="companion">Đi cùng NPC</button></div>`)}else html+='<p>Tiến gần một người dân để mở hoạt động quan hệ.</p>';
   html+=block('Liên kết đã ghi nhớ',Object.keys(d.people).slice(0,12).map(k=>`<p>♥ ${esc(k.replace(/::/g,' · '))}: ${d.people[k].trust}/5</p>`).join('')||'<p class="muted">Chưa có ký ức.</p>');
  }else if(tab==='study'){
   html=`<h3>Adaptive English Adventure</h3><p>${esc(cfg?.example||'Giao tiếp cùng NPC để học tiếng Anh theo tình huống.')}</p>`+block('Luyện viết / diễn đạt',`<p>Nhập câu tiếng Anh liên quan đến tình huống đang chơi. Hệ thống kiểm tra theo quy tắc, không giả làm bài chấm AI.</p><textarea id="lq60-answer" aria-label="English sentence" placeholder="Type your English response here..."></textarea><div class="actions"><button data-v60="study:check">Kiểm tra và lưu tiến độ</button></div>`)+block('Tiến độ học tập',`<p>${d.learning.entries.length} lượt luyện · ${Object.keys(d.learning.words).length} từ khác nhau đã ghi nhận</p><p class="muted">${Object.entries(d.learning.words).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,18).map(([w,n])=>esc(w)+' ('+n+')').join(' · ')}</p>`);
  }else if(tab==='museum'){
   const art=Object.entries(d.artifacts),insideMuseum=aid==='english-town'&&sc?.inside?.b?.name==='Library';
   html='<h3>Adventure Memory Museum</h3><p>Tất cả kỷ vật quan trọng được lưu vào bảo tàng hành trình. Trở lại thư viện English Town để mở căn phòng ký ức.</p>'+
    block('Bộ sưu tập ('+art.length+')',art.map(([id,v])=>`<p>✦ <strong>${esc(v.title)}</strong> — ${esc(v.desc||'Ký ức trong hành trình')}</p>`).join('')||'<p class="muted">Hoàn thành nhiệm vụ hoặc đối chiếu Time Echo để nhận kỷ vật.</p>')+
    (insideMuseum?'<div class="actions"><button data-v60="museum:visit">🏛 Mở phòng trưng bày</button></div>':'<p class="muted">Vị trí: English Town → Library.</p>');
  }else if(tab==='settings'){
   html='<h3>LifeQuest Core 2.0 — Diagnostics</h3>'+block('Dữ liệu & hiệu năng',`<p>Version ${VERSION} · ${Object.keys(d.regions).length} vùng đã khởi tạo · ${d.events.length} sự kiện lịch sử</p><p>Lưu trạng thái: ${saveFault?'Không ghi được':'Đã kết nối kho lưu hiện có'}</p><div class="actions"><button data-v60="settings:quiet">${d.preferences.quiet?'Bật lại':'Giảm'} hoạt cảnh V60</button><button data-v60="settings:backup">Xuất bản sao lưu V60</button><button data-v60="settings:inspect">Kiểm tra vị trí hiện tại</button></div>`)+
    block('Lưu ý',`<p>V60 giữ nguyên hệ cửa và collision V59. Hệ Time Echo, nhiệm vụ theo vùng, NPC Memory và Companion được phát triển độc lập, không sửa hay ghi đè tiến độ cũ.</p>`);
  }
  dialogue.innerHTML=html;
 }
 function atQuestLocation(sc){const cfg=MAPS[areaId(sc)];if(!cfg)return false;
  if(sc.inside)return sc.inside.b?.name===cfg.building;
  return nearbyBuilding(sc)?.name===cfg.building;
 }
 function showTask(sc){const m=mission(sc);if(!m){toast('Hãy tới một trong tám map chính.');return}if(!atQuestLocation(sc)){toast('Hãy đến '+m.cfg.building+' tại '+m.cfg.name+' để tham gia nhiệm vụ.');tab='quests';togglePanel(true);return}if(m.r.complete){toast('Công trình này đã hoàn thành! Hãy ghé bảo tàng ký ức.');return}
  tab='quests';togglePanel(true);if(m.r.progress===0){
   dialogue.innerHTML=`<h3>${esc(m.cfg.steps[0])}</h3><div class="card"><p>Hãy xem kỹ các chi tiết trên vật chứng tại ${esc(m.cfg.building)}. Mỗi vật chứng có một hình dáng riêng phù hợp với vùng đất.</p><button data-v60="detail:open">🔎 Mở chế độ quan sát vật chứng</button></div>`;
  }else if(m.r.progress===1){
   pending={kind:'sequence',chosen:[]};sequenceUI(sc);
  }else if(m.r.progress===2){
   pending={kind:'speak'};
   dialogue.innerHTML=`<h3>${esc(m.cfg.steps[2])}</h3><p>Hãy diễn đạt một câu tiếng Anh phù hợp với tình huống. Câu không nhất thiết phải giống ví dụ.</p><div class="card"><p>Gợi ý: <em>${esc(m.cfg.example)}</em></p><textarea id="lq60-task-sentence" placeholder="Write your sentence in English..."></textarea><div class="actions"><button data-v60="task:speak">Gửi lời giải thích</button><button data-v60="task:back">Quay lại</button></div></div>`;
  }
 }
 const INSPECT_LABELS={
  'english-town':['Dấu niêm phong','Tên người nhận','Ngày gửi'],
  'mining-town':['Ngọn đèn Elias','Dấu nhà ga','Ký hiệu tuyến'],
  'english-town-past':['Dấu của Tom','Tờ thư','Túi bưu kiện'],
  'art-district':['Phối màu','Chữ ký họa sĩ','Thông điệp bức tranh'],
  'tech-co':['Trạng thái màn hình','Tuyến dữ liệu','Bản ghi hệ thống'],
  'old-oriaris':['Huy hiệu hoàng gia','Tiếng chuông','Vết mực cổ'],
  'wish-valley':['Lá thảo dược','Rễ cây cổ','Biểu tượng Thác Mẹ'],
  'vocalt-greedy-land':['Vỏ đồng hồ','Bánh răng','Lời khắc bên trong']

 function drawArtifact(canvas,aid){const c=canvas.getContext('2d'),cfg=MAPS[aid],ink='#15243a',gold='#e7bf78',paper='#d9bb86';
  c.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;const rect=(x,y,w,h,col)=>{c.fillStyle=col;c.fillRect(x,y,w,h)},line=(x,y,x2,y2,col,width=2)=>{c.strokeStyle=col;c.lineWidth=width;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(x2,y2);c.stroke()};
  rect(0,0,256,140,'#102333');for(let i=0;i<16;i++){rect((i*37)%250,(i*17)%133,3,3,i%2?'#274a57':'#183b48')}
  rect(16,15,224,111,'#293c48');rect(20,19,216,103,'#394b53');
  if(aid==='english-town'||aid==='english-town-past'){
   rect(72,22,114,95,'#604c3b');rect(76,18,112,96,paper);rect(82,25,100,81,'#edd7a7');
   for(let y=48;y<99;y+=12)rect(91,y,81,3,'#9c8066');rect(123,75,18,18,'#ad5550');rect(129,81,6,6,gold);rect(88,33,44,5,'#8f6c4d');
  }else if(aid==='mining-town'){
   rect(118,25,20,14,'#846849');rect(110,37,36,65,'#172f3e');rect(117,39,22,56,'#b8d9b4');rect(121,50,14,32,'#72dfcc');rect(124,56,8,22,'#fff4c3');rect(113,101,29,9,'#715340');
   for(let i=0;i<4;i++)rect(73+i*28,110,19,4,'#6b8791');
  }else if(aid==='art-district'){
   rect(82,23,94,92,'#d3b7a2');rect(88,29,82,80,'#edd8b7');
   const clr=['#e5829f','#8cd0e2','#f9ce8d','#a5b3e5','#91c99b'];for(let i=0;i<clr.length;i++){c.fillStyle=clr[i];c.fillRect(96+i*13,45+(i%2)*13,13,19)}
   line(106,101,165,57,'#5a3b3d',5);rect(159,48,18,8,'#e6e9c8');
  }else if(aid==='tech-co'){
   rect(62,30,132,75,'#0c2636');rect(68,36,120,63,'#2b6d85');for(let i=0;i<7;i++){line(78,45+i*7,170,45+i*7,i%2?'#92d8c7':'#3e9bad',2)}
   rect(99,110,58,7,'#627a92');rect(112,105,32,5,'#223d50');rect(139,65,20,17,'#f4c57f');
  }else if(aid==='old-oriaris'){
   rect(87,28,82,79,'#63434b');rect(95,32,66,69,'#a67576');c.fillStyle='#d5b486';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(128,25);c.lineTo(160,47);c.lineTo(147,93);c.lineTo(109,93);c.lineTo(97,47);c.closePath();c.fill();
   rect(120,48,16,33,'#8b5362');rect(126,39,4,49,'#ffe2a7');
  }else if(aid==='wish-valley'){
   rect(72,79,111,28,'#8d7146');rect(82,71,93,13,'#b5a174');for(let i=0;i<7;i++){let x=94+i*12;line(x,75,x+(i%2?8:-8),42-i%3*6,'#4f8d58',4);rect(x-5,29-i%3*5,10,13,i%2?'#8fb46d':'#b7d58d')}
   rect(113,86,30,9,'#d4be86');
  }else if(aid==='vocalt-greedy-land'){
   c.fillStyle='#b88f5b';c.beginPath();c.arc(129,70,47,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();c.fillStyle='#4f3b42';c.beginPath();c.arc(129,70,39,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();
   for(let i=0;i<12;i++){let a=i*Math.PI/6;rect(126+Math.sin(a)*31,68-Math.cos(a)*31,4,4,gold)}line(129,70,145,54,gold,3);line(129,70,128,40,gold,3);
  }
  rect(23,21,12,4,gold);rect(221,110,12,4,gold);rect(21,119,214,2,'#4c6e75');
 }
 function showDetailView(sc){const cfg=MAPS[areaId(sc)];if(!cfg)return;pending={kind:'inspect',found:[]};
  dialogue.innerHTML=`<h3>🔎 Discovery Camera — ${esc(cfg.artifact)}</h3><p>Quan sát ba chi tiết quan trọng trước khi kết luận.</p><div class="card" style="text-align:center"><canvas id="lq60-art-canvas" width="256" height="140" style="display:block;width:min(100%,512px);margin:auto;image-rendering:pixelated;border:2px solid #607b83;border-radius:8px" aria-label="Mô hình pixel-art vật chứng"></canvas></div><div id="lq60-observation"></div>`;
  drawArtifact(document.getElementById('lq60-art-canvas'),areaId(sc));detailButtons(sc);
 }
 function detailButtons(sc){if(pending?.kind!=='inspect')return;const items=INSPECT_LABELS[areaId(sc)]||['Hình dáng','Dấu hiệu','Thông tin'];
  const el=document.getElementById('lq60-observation');if(!el)return;
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="card"><p>Đã quan sát ${pending.found.length}/3 chi tiết</p><div class="actions">${items.map((l,i)=>`<button data-v60="detail:spot:${i}" ${pending.found.includes(i)?'disabled':''}>${pending.found.includes(i)?'✓ ':''}${esc(l)}</button>`).join('')}</div>${pending.found.length===3?'<p class="done">Đủ ba manh mối. Có thể ghi vào nhật ký.</p><button data-v60="detail:finish">✓ Ghi nhận vật chứng</button>':''}</div>`;
 }
 function sequenceUI(sc){const m=mission(sc),p=pending;if(!m||!p)return;
  dialogue.innerHTML=`<h3>${esc(m.cfg.steps[1])}</h3><p>Chọn theo đúng trình tự để hoàn thành công việc. Kéo thả không bắt buộc; thao tác được bằng chuột hoặc bàn phím.</p><div class="card"><p>Đã chọn: ${p.chosen.map(i=>esc(m.cfg.items[i])).join(' → ')||'Chưa có'}</p><div class="actions">${m.cfg.items.map((w,i)=>`<button data-v60="sequence:${i}" ${p.chosen.includes(i)?'disabled':''}>${esc(w)}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="muted">Hãy bắt đầu từ bước chuẩn bị, sau đó thực hiện và cuối cùng xác nhận kết quả.</p><button data-v60="sequence:reset">Làm lại</button></div>`;
 }
 function viewMuseum(sc){const d=data(sc),art=Object.values(d.artifacts);d.museum.opened=true;persist(sc);tab='museum';togglePanel(true);
  dialogue.innerHTML='<h3>🏛 The Memory Hall</h3><p>Những hành trình chỉ trở nên trọn vẹn khi chúng ta nhớ được người đã gặp và những việc đã làm.</p>'+
    '<div class="card">'+(art.length?art.map((a,i)=>`<p>◈ ${i+1}. <strong>${esc(a.title)}</strong> — ${esc(a.desc)}</p>`).join(''):'<p>Những chiếc kệ còn trống. Hãy khám phá thế giới để đưa những câu chuyện về đây.</p>')+'</div><button data-v60="tab:overview">Trở lại hành trình</button>';
 }
 function interact(){const sc=scene();if(!valid(sc))return;const obj=currentObject(sc);if(!obj){toast('Hãy đến gần công trình quan trọng, vật chứng hoặc NPC.');return}
  if(obj.kind==='museum'){viewMuseum(sc);return}
  if(obj.kind==='echo'){const result=echoInteract(sc,obj.echo);tab='echo';togglePanel(true);paintPanel();toast(result?'Đã ghép đủ hai vật chứng và nhận kỷ vật!':'Đã lưu manh mối; tìm nửa còn lại ở vùng khác.');return}
  if(obj.kind==='npc'){tab='people';togglePanel(true);return}
  if(obj.kind==='mission'||obj.kind==='building'){showTask(sc);return}
 }
 function action(cmd){const sc=scene();if(cmd==='close'){togglePanel(false);return}if(cmd.startsWith('tab:')){tab=cmd.slice(4);pending=null;paintPanel();return}
  if(!valid(sc))return;if(cmd==='interact'){interact();return}
  if(cmd==='mission'){showTask(sc);return}
  if(cmd==='task:back'){paintPanel();return}
  if(cmd==='detail:open'){if(!atQuestLocation(sc))return;showDetailView(sc);return}
  if(cmd.startsWith('detail:spot:')){if(!atQuestLocation(sc)||pending?.kind!=='inspect')return;const i=Number(cmd.slice(12));if(i>=0&&i<=2&&!pending.found.includes(i))pending.found.push(i);detailButtons(sc);return}
  if(cmd==='detail:finish'){if(!atQuestLocation(sc)||pending?.kind!=='inspect'||pending.found.length!==3)return;pending=null;missionAdvance(sc,'inspect');showTask(sc);return}
  if(cmd==='task:speak'){if(!atQuestLocation(sc))return;const v=document.getElementById('lq60-task-sentence')?.value||'',res=study(sc,v);if(!res.ok){toast(res.message);return}missionAdvance(sc,'speak');toast('✓ Nhiệm vụ hoàn thành: công trình đã thay đổi!');paintPanel();return}
  if(cmd.startsWith('sequence:')){
   if(!atQuestLocation(sc))return;const w=cmd.slice(9);if(!pending||pending.kind!=='sequence')return;
   if(w==='reset'){pending.chosen=[];sequenceUI(sc);return}
   const i=Number(w),m=mission(sc);if(!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>2||!m)return;
   if(i!==m.cfg.order[pending.chosen.length]){pending.chosen=[];toast('Chưa đúng trình tự. Hãy thử lại từ bước chuẩn bị.');sequenceUI(sc);return}
   pending.chosen.push(i);if(pending.chosen.length===3){missionAdvance(sc,'assemble');pending=null;showTask(sc)}else sequenceUI(sc);return;
  }
  if(cmd==='study:check'){const res=study(sc,document.getElementById('lq60-answer')?.value);toast(res.message);if(res.ok)paintPanel();return}
  if(cmd==='npc:help'||cmd==='npc:story'){const n=nearestNPC(sc,115);if(!n){toast('Không có NPC gần bạn.');return}const t=cmd.endsWith('help')?'Đề nghị hỗ trợ':'Tìm hiểu câu chuyện';const r=gossip(sc,n,t);toast(r.newTopic?`Đã ghi nhớ mối quan hệ. Độ tin tưởng: ${r.trust}/5`:'NPC nhớ lần trò chuyện này.');paintPanel();return}
  if(cmd==='companion'){const r=guide(sc);toast(r.message);paintPanel();return}
  if(cmd==='echo:inspect'){interact();return}
  if(cmd==='museum:visit'){viewMuseum(sc);return}
  if(cmd==='settings:quiet'){data(sc).preferences.quiet=!data(sc).preferences.quiet;persist(sc);paintPanel();return}
  if(cmd==='settings:inspect'){const c={area:areaId(sc),room:roomId(sc),player:[Math.round(sc.player.x),Math.round(sc.player.y)],blocked:!!sc.blocked(sc.player.x,sc.player.y),world:sc.inside?'interior':'outdoor'};toast(JSON.stringify(c));return}
  if(cmd==='settings:backup'){const d=data(sc);const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify({product:'LifeQuest V60',version:VERSION,world:d},null,2)],{type:'application/json'});const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='LifeQuest_V60_World_Backup.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);return}
 }
 function boot(){createUI();renderHUD();}
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
 return Object.freeze({VERSION,MAPS,TRAILS,ECHO,preEnter,paintBack,paintFront,onEnter,onLeave,onArea,update,interact,missionAdvance,pulse,relation,gossip,echoInteract,echoState,study,mission,interiorAnchor,guide,clearFollower,publish,subscribe,region,record,data,save: persist,refreshScene,open:()=>togglePanel(true),test:{roomId,clock,phase,nearbyBuilding,sequence:()=>pending?.chosen||[],stats:sc=>({regions:progress(sc),echoes:totalEchoes(sc),people:Object.keys(data(sc).people).length,artifacts:Object.keys(data(sc).artifacts).length})}});
})();
window.LQWorldV60 = LQ60;
/* LIFEQUEST AI V61 — ART DIRECTION ENGINE
 * Additive graphics only. DOES NOT change collisions, doors, navmesh, NPC sprites,
 * quests or saves. Uses existing Phaser Graphics and existing room definitions.
 * Material, locale and scene time drive a bounded, deterministic render system.
 */
const LQ61 = (() => {
  'use strict';
  const TILE=32, VERSION='61.0.0';
  const REGIONS={
    'english-town':{key:'civic',name:'English Town',base:0x3b6867,light:0xffe5ae,dark:0x193940,material:'stone',wind:.3},
    'mining-town':{key:'mining',name:'Minerals Town',base:0x355565,light:0xfcc58a,dark:0x1b2b39,material:'metal',wind:.24},
    'english-town-past':{key:'past',name:'Oriaris 70 Years Ago',base:0x937754,light:0xffda9e,dark:0x42352a,material:'timber',wind:.4},
    'oriaris-trail-1':{key:'forest',name:'North Trail I',base:0x476b4f,light:0xf0e9aa,dark:0x1c362e,material:'earth',wind:.48},
    'oriaris-trail-2':{key:'forest',name:'North Trail II',base:0x476b4f,light:0xf0e9aa,dark:0x1c362e,material:'earth',wind:.57},
    'oriaris-trail-3':{key:'forest',name:'North Trail III',base:0x42694c,light:0xf0e9aa,dark:0x1b322d,material:'earth',wind:.53},

  function graphics(g){return{
    r:(x,y,w,h,col,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0)g.fillStyle(col,a).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h))},
    o:(x,y,w,h,col,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0)g.fillStyle(col,a).fillEllipse(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h))},
    c:(x,y,r,col,a=1)=>{if(r>0)g.fillStyle(col,a).fillCircle(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),r)},
    l:(x,y,xx,yy,col,a=.8,th=2)=>g.lineStyle(th,col,a).lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(xx),Math.round(yy)),
    tri:(x,y,x2,y2,x3,y3,col,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(col,a).fillTriangle(x,y,x2,y2,x3,y3),
    arc:(x,y,r,col,a=.8)=>g.lineStyle(2,col,a).strokeCircle(x,y,r),
  }}

  const done=(s)=>!!(s?.__lq60?.complete||s?.__lq58?.complete);

  // Localized details are painted over existing object's INSIDE ITS ORIGINAL HITBOX.
  function itemDetail(g,item,s){if(!item||!s?.__lq58||!Number.isFinite(item.x))return;const a=room(s),b=graphics(g),x=item.x*32,y=item.y*32,w=item.w*32,h=item.h*32,k=String(item.k||'').toLowerCase();if(w<25||h<22)return;
    const w0=Math.max(1,w-14),h0=Math.max(1,h-12);
    const plate=(color)=>{b.r(x+8,y+9,w0,h0,0x0e1a23,.36);b.r(x+7,y+7,w0,h0,color,.52);b.l(x+10,y+10,x+w-12,y+10,0xffe7c2,.35,2)};
    if(/desk|counter|table|workbench|terminal|anvil/.test(k)){
      plate(a.kind==='mining'?0x8c9792:a.kind==='valley'?0x9f865c:0xab8064);
      const n=Math.max(1,Math.floor((w-21)/19));for(let i=0;i<n;i++){let xx=x+13+i*(w-26)/n;const col=a.kind==='tech'?0x86d8db:a.kind==='mining'?0xd4b282:a.kind==='valley'?0x9fd08c:0xf2d9a7;b.r(xx,y+14,Math.min(12,(w-26)/n-2),6,col,.76);b.r(xx+3,y+16,3,2,0xfff5cd,.45)}
      b.r(x+11,y+h-7,7,4,0x29333b,.78);b.r(x+w-19,y+h-7,7,4,0x29333b,.78);
    }else if(/clock|gear|mechanism/.test(k)){clockface(b,x+w/2,y+h/2,Math.min(w,h)*.32,.6)}
    else if(/forge|oven|stove|fireplace/.test(k)){plate(0x684c45);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let xx=x+w*.3+i*w*.10;b.r(xx,y+h*.48-8-i%2*5,Math.max(3,w*.065),13+i%2*5,i%2?0xffbd70:0xe47a54,.73)}b.o(x+w/2,y+h-7,w*.72,8,0xffd28d,.1)}
    else if(/shelf|rack|cabinet|bookcase|display|case/.test(k)){plate(0x775c4e);for(let i=0;i<2;i++){let yy=y+15+i*(h-30)*.56;b.r(x+9,yy,w-18,3,0xd1ab79,.79);for(let j=0;j<Math.min(9,Math.floor(w/13));j++){let xx=x+12+j*(w-25)/Math.max(1,Math.min(9,Math.floor(w/13)));b.r(xx,yy-8,5,8,[0x789e99,0xe0af76,0x8f9fc1,0xd29fae][(j+i)%4],.81)}}}
    else if(/bed|sofa|bench|couch/.test(k)){plate(a.kind==='valley'?0x9aa785:a.kind==='royal'?0x9d718a:0x8e9390);b.r(x+10,y+9,w*.35,8,0xf6e7c5,.75);for(let j=0;j<5;j++)b.l(x+8+j*8,y+h-12,x+18+j*8,y+h-7,0xd3b896,.34,1)}
    else if(/plant|herbs|tree/.test(k)){leaf(b,x+w*.5,y+h*.47,safe(Math.min(w,h)/32,.5,1.4),a.id%7)}
  }
  function architectureFront(g,s){if(!s?.__lq58)return;const a=room(s),b=graphics(g),W=a.w,H=a.h,kind=a.kind;
    // Front edge with visual doorway, never painted across lower central route.

  }
  function indoorAccent(sc,ms){if(!sc?.inside||!sc.add?.graphics)return;
    const identifier=(sc.area?.id||'')+'|'+(sc.inside?.b?.name||'')+'|'+sc.inside.floor;
    if(roomRef!==identifier||!indoorG){roomRef=identifier;indoorG=null;const g=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(8.85);sc.inside.objs.push(g);indoorG=g;}
    const g=indoorG;g.clear();if(!prefs.light&&!prefs.wind)return;const spec=sc.inside.spec;if(!spec?.__lq58)return;
    const b=graphics(g),a=room(spec),unit=32*(sc.inside.k||1),ox=(typeof Yr!=='undefined'?Yr:20000),oy=(typeof Xr!=='undefined'?Xr:0),K=sc.inside.k||1,t=sc.time?.now*.001||0,p=clock(sc);
    const px=x=>ox+x*K,py=y=>oy+y*K;
    const glow=(x,y,r,col,al)=>{if(!prefs.light)return;b.o(px(x),py(y),r*K,r*.38*K,col,al)};
    if(prefs.light){const c=p.night?0xffc77a:p.dusk?0xffddb0:0xfff4c9,alpha=p.night?.21:p.dusk?.13:.09;

    }
    if(prefs.wind&&prefs.quality!=='performance'){
      const wave=Math.sin(t*.9+a.id%10);
      if(a.kind==='market'){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const x=px(a.w*(.2+i*.2)),y=py(41+i%2*8);b.l(x,y,x+5*K*wave,y+10*K,0xffdb98,.27,2)}}
      else if(a.kind==='valley'){for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let x=px(30+i*(a.w-60)/4),y=py(58+i%2*9);b.o(x+wave*5*K,y,7*K,3*K,0xbde9a5,.31)}}
      else if(a.kind==='mining'){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){let x=px(42+i*(a.w-84)/2),y=py(68+(Math.sin(t+i)*4));b.r(x,y,3*K,2*K,0xffd593,.53)}}
      else if(a.kind==='royal'){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=px(a.w*.25+i*a.w*.25),y=py(62);b.r(x,y+Math.sin(t+i)*2*K,3*K,9*K,0xffcb8d,.27)}}
    }
  }
  function outdoors(sc,dt){if(!sc?.area||!sc.player||!sc.add?.graphics)return;
    const id=sc.area.id,R=theme(id),P=clock(sc),Q=QUALITY[prefs.quality],x0=sc.player.x,y0=sc.player.y,t=(sc.time?.now||0)/1000;
    if(!outdoorG||outRef!==id||sceneRef!==sc){if(outdoorG)try{outdoorG.destroy()}catch{};if(debugG)try{debugG.destroy()}catch{};outdoorG=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(7.65);debugG=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(17.4);outRef=id;sceneRef=sc}
    const g=outdoorG,b=graphics(g);g.clear();const gd=debugG;gd.clear();const tiles=sc.area;
    // Strict visible-area effect cap: no persistent world object/collider created.
    let drawn=0;const density=QUALITY[prefs.quality].limit,span=prefs.quality==='cinematic'?98:112;
    const lx=Math.floor((x0-440)/span),hx=Math.ceil((x0+440)/span),ly=Math.floor((y0-305)/span),hy=Math.ceil((y0+305)/span);
    for(let yy=ly;yy<=hy&&drawn<density;yy++)for(let xx=lx;xx<=hx&&drawn<density;xx++){
      let z=seeded(xx,yy,hash(id)),px=xx*span+15+Math.floor(z*75),py=yy*span+21+Math.floor(seeded(yy,xx,hash(id))*59);
      const gx=Math.floor(px/32),gy=Math.floor(py/32);
      if(gx<1||gy<1||gx>=(tiles.w||100)-1||gy>=(tiles.h||100)-1)continue;
      // Never place scenery over a building's circulation footprint or on a warp.
      if(tiles.warpAt?.(gx,gy)||tiles.isTileBlocked?.(gx,gy))continue;
      let road=!!tiles.isRoad?.(gx,gy),a=.20+.11*Math.sin(t*.8+z*16),motion=prefs.wind?Math.sin(t*R.wind+z*11)*4:0;
      if(R.key==='valley'||R.key==='forest'){
        if(prefs.particles){b.o(px,py+2,11,4,0x122c29,.16);if(!road)b.o(px,py+motion*.35,13,6,z>.5?0x90bc76:0x70a16b,.3)}
        if(prefs.wind){b.l(px-3,py-3,px+motion,py-13,R.light,.3,1);b.c(px+motion,py-14,2.1,R.light,.32)}
      }else if(R.key==='mining'){
        if(prefs.particles){b.r(px-6,py,12,3,0x273b45,.3);b.r(px+motion*.12,py-7,3,3,z>.5?0x9be2d6:0xe2ae76,.36)}
        if(prefs.light)b.o(px,py-8,15,7,R.light,.065);
      }else if(R.key==='tech'){
        if(prefs.particles){b.r(px,py,10,2,0x7fd4d9,.2);b.r(px+5,py-4,2,3,0x9ef8e9,.34)}
      }else if(R.key==='royal'){
        if(prefs.particles&&!road){b.r(px-5,py-5,2,12,0x5f4d6e,.28);b.c(px,py,2,0xffd7a8,.27)}}
      else if(R.key==='market'||R.key==='art'){
        if(prefs.wind){b.l(px,py,px+motion,py-8,R.light,.3,1)}if(prefs.particles)b.r(px+4,py,4,2,R.light,.23)
      }else{if(prefs.particles&&!road){b.r(px-5,py+2,10,2,R.light,.15);b.r(px+motion,py-1,2,2,R.light,.3)}}
      drawn++;
    }
    // Physically plausible camera-local water ripples when a mapped water tile is present.
    if(prefs.water&&prefs.quality!=='performance'){
      let count=0;for(let yy=Math.floor((y0-180)/32);yy<=Math.floor((y0+180)/32)&&count<7;yy+=3){for(let xx=Math.floor((x0-225)/32);xx<=Math.floor((x0+225)/32)&&count<7;xx+=4){
        if(xx<0||yy<0||xx>=tiles.w||yy>=tiles.h)continue;
        const tile=tiles.tiles?.[yy]?.[xx];let water=typeof tiles.isWater==='function'?tiles.isWater(xx,yy):false;
        if(!water&&tile)water=/water|river|stream|lake/.test(String(tile.kind||tile.type||''));
        if(!water)continue;const xxp=xx*32+16,yyp=yy*32+16;let d=(t*.55+(xx+yy)*.13)%1;
        g.lineStyle(1,R.light,.07*(1-d)).strokeEllipse(xxp,yyp,8+d*17,2+d*5);count++;
      }}
    }
    // Contact shadow is intentionally below player sprite, drawn as a 1-ellipse overlay.
    if(prefs.light){b.o(x0,y0+10,23,7,R.dark,.2)}
    if(prefs.debug){
      gd.lineStyle(1,0xffd66f,.9).strokeRect(Math.floor(x0/32)*32,Math.floor(y0/32)*32,32,32);
      const buildings=(sc.area.buildings||[]).filter(it=>Math.hypot((it.x+it.w/2)*32-x0,(it.y+it.h/2)*32-y0)<350).slice(0,12);
      for(const obj of buildings){gd.lineStyle(1,0xf3c675,.36).strokeRect(obj.x*32,obj.y*32,obj.w*32,obj.h*32);gd.fillStyle(0x9fffe4,.8).fillCircle((obj.door+.5)*32,(obj.y+obj.h+.5)*32,3)}
    }
    drawStats.objects=drawn;drawStats.particles=drawn;
  }
