 const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
 const hash=s=>{let n=2166136261;for(const c of String(s)){n=Math.imul(n^c.charCodeAt(0),16777619)}return n>>>0};
 const H=(s,n)=>((hash(s+'_'+n)%10000)/10000);
 const gfx=g=>({
  r:(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0)g.fillStyle(c,a).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h))},
  e:(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0)g.fillStyle(c,a).fillEllipse(x,y,w,h)},
  c:(x,y,r,c,a=1)=>{if(r>0)g.fillStyle(c,a).fillCircle(x,y,r)},
  l:(x,y,xx,yy,c,a=.8,t=2)=>g.lineStyle(t,c,a).lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(xx),Math.round(yy)),
  tri:(x,y,x2,y2,x3,y3,c,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillTriangle(x,y,x2,y2,x3,y3),
  ring:(x,y,r,c,a=.8,t=2)=>g.lineStyle(t,c,a).strokeCircle(x,y,r),
 });
 const roomKey=s=>String(s?.__lq58?.area||'english-town')+'|'+String(s?.__lq58?.building||'room')+'|'+String(s?.__lq58?.floor||0);
 const style=s=>STYLE[s?.__lq58?.area]||STYLE['english-town'];
 function identity(s){const name=String(s?.__lq58?.building||s?.__lq60?.building||'').toLowerCase(),region=style(s).id;
  if(region==='roots'&&/vaela/.test(name))return 'vaela';
  if(region==='mine'&&/great mine|workshop|freight/.test(name))return 'mine';
  if(region==='royal'&&/opera/.test(name))return 'opera';
  if(region==='guild'&&/chronos|clock/.test(name))return 'chronos';
  return region;
 }
 function occupational(s){const k=identity(s),n=String(s?.__lq58?.building||'').toLowerCase();
  if(/bakery|baker|cafe|kitchen/.test(n))return 'kitchen';
  if(/academy|library|archive|school/.test(n))return 'archive';
  if(/market|bazaar|exchange|stall|shop|jewel|cloth|smith|craft/.test(n))return 'trade';
  if(/house|home|cottage|hut/.test(n))return 'home';
  if(/office|nexus|lab|data/.test(n))return 'office';
  if(['opera','mine','vaela','chronos'].includes(k))return signature[k].role;
  return 'community';
 }
 const memo=new WeakMap();const visits=new Map();
 function plan(s){if(!s||!s.__lq58)return null;if(memo.has(s))return memo.get(s);
  const key=roomKey(s),id=identity(s),p=style(s),seed=hash(key),W=s.w*T,HH=s.h*T,role=occupational(s);
  const styleId=p.id,variant=seed%5;
  // Decor motifs are computed from room role and geometry, not random clutter.
  const items=(s.items||[]).filter(i=>Number.isFinite(i.x)&&Number.isFinite(i.y)&&Number.isFinite(i.w)&&Number.isFinite(i.h));
  const occupied=items.map(i=>({x:i.x*T,y:i.y*T,w:i.w*T,h:i.h*T,kind:i.k||''}));
  const safeZones=[]; const walkX=W/2, doorSafe={x:walkX-32,y:HH-117,w:64,h:112};
  const rects=[
   {x:W*.08,y:HH*.32,w:W*.25,h:HH*.31,kind:'left'},
   {x:W*.67,y:HH*.32,w:W*.25,h:HH*.31,kind:'right'},
   {x:W*.32,y:HH*.39,w:W*.36,h:HH*.22,kind:'center'},
   {x:W*.09,y:HH*.67,w:W*.22,h:HH*.15,kind:'lowerLeft'},
   {x:W*.68,y:HH*.67,w:W*.22,h:HH*.15,kind:'lowerRight'}];
  for(const r of rects){let hits=0;for(const o of occupied){const sx=Math.max(r.x,o.x),ex=Math.min(r.x+r.w,o.x+o.w),sy=Math.max(r.y,o.y),ey=Math.min(r.y+r.h,o.y+o.h);if(ex>sx&&ey>sy)hits+=(ex-sx)*(ey-sy)}
   const ratio=hits/(r.w*r.h);if(ratio<.45&&r.y+r.h<HH-55&&!(r.x<doorSafe.x+doorSafe.w&&r.x+r.w>doorSafe.x&&r.y<doorSafe.y+doorSafe.h&&r.y+r.h>doorSafe.y))safeZones.push({...r,free:1-ratio});
  }
  const main=['roots','mine','heritage','forest','art','tech','royal','guild','civic'].indexOf(styleId);
  const shape=signature[id]?.shape||['split','parallel','offset','angled','zigzag'][variant];
  const motif=seed%7;
  const itemBias=occupied.length?Math.round((occupied.reduce((a,o)=>a+o.x+o.w/2,0)/occupied.length)/Math.max(1,W)*4):0;
  const footprint=[shape,role,Math.round(W/128),Math.round(HH/128),occupied.length<4?'low':occupied.length<10?'mid':'high',main,`motif${motif}`,`bias${itemBias}`].join('|');
  const out={key,id,p,role,shape,variant,motif,W,H:HH,occupied,safeZones,seed,items,signature:signature[id]||null,footprint,building:String(s.__lq58.building||'')};memo.set(s,out);visits.set(key,out);return out;
 }
 function ornament(b,x,y,kind,p,scale=1){let f=scale; if(kind==='plant'){
   b.r(x-2*f,y,4*f,15*f,p.deep,.84);for(let j=0;j<5;j++)b.e(x+Math.sin(j*2.5)*8*f,y-11*f-(j%2)*8*f,12*f,9*f,j%2?0x92bc79:0x6f9e74,.9);
   b.c(x+3*f,y-18*f,2*f,p.shine,.7);
  }else if(kind==='lamp'){
   b.r(x-2*f,y-15*f,4*f,18*f,p.deep);b.r(x-8*f,y-20*f,16*f,15*f,p.trim);b.r(x-5*f,y-17*f,10*f,8*f,0xffdf9e,.92);b.e(x,y+2*f,28*f,7*f,p.trim,.16);
  }else if(kind==='dial'){
   b.c(x+1*f,y+2*f,12*f,p.deep,.6);b.c(x,y,11*f,p.trim);b.c(x,y,8*f,0x70503f);b.l(x,y,x+4*f,y-5*f,p.shine,.9,2*f);b.l(x,y,x-4*f,y+2*f,p.shine,.9,1*f);
  }else if(kind==='column'){
   b.r(x-9*f,y-2*f,18*f,29*f,p.deep);b.r(x-5*f,y,10*f,27*f,p.trim,.65);b.r(x-12*f,y-5*f,24*f,6*f,p.shine,.88);
  }else if(kind==='gear'){
   b.c(x,y,13*f,p.deep);for(let i=0;i<8;i++){let a=i*Math.PI/4;b.r(x+Math.cos(a)*12*f-3*f,y+Math.sin(a)*12*f-3*f,6*f,6*f,p.trim)}b.c(x,y,9*f,p.trim);b.c(x,y,4*f,p.deep);
  }
 }
 const safeUnit=(v,min,max)=>clamp(Math.round(v),min,max);
 function drawBack(g,s){if(!prefs.enabled)return;let m=plan(s);if(!m)return;
  const b=gfx(g),{W,H:HH,p,variant,id,shape,role}=m,cx=W/2,top=98,bot=HH-49,fh=bot-top;
  if(fh<72||W<200)return;
  // Floor composition: 1 primary motive, side counterpoint, intentionally quiet central travel lane.
  const inset=20;
  b.r(inset,top,W-inset*2,5,p.deep,.57);b.r(inset+6,top+4,W-inset*2-12,2,p.trim,.3);
  if(id==='vaela'||p.id==='roots'){
   b.e(cx,top+fh*.48,Math.min(W*.74,370),fh*.72,p.deep,.65);
   b.e(cx,top+fh*.48,Math.min(W*.66,338),fh*.64,0xa5905e,.69);
   b.e(cx,top+fh*.48,Math.min(W*.58,304),fh*.54,0xc6ad72,.56);
   for(let i=0;i<12;i++){let a=i*Math.PI/6,mx=cx+Math.cos(a)*W*.27,my=top+fh*.48+Math.sin(a)*fh*.29;
    b.l(cx+Math.cos(a)*43,top+fh*.48+Math.sin(a)*20,mx,my,0x5e503a,.42,3);}
   b.e(cx,top+fh*.47,82,53,0x664b34,.83);b.e(cx,top+fh*.47,69,39,0x8a613b,.88);
   // Branch-like nested loggias, a different shape from the rectangular industrial halls.
   for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let xx=32+i*(W-64)/3;b.e(xx,76,67,39,p.deep,.74);b.e(xx,69,55,31,i%2?0x6b9668:0x91b46d,.84);
    ornament(b,xx,91,'plant',p,.75);}
   b.l(18,45,84,100,0x6c8151,.73,8);b.l(W-18,45,W-84,100,0x6c8151,.73,8);
  }else if(id==='mine'||p.id==='mine'){
   // Three functional machine bays with visual connecting rails; machines never create phantom collisions.
   const s1=W*.26,s2=W*.74;
   for(let j=0;j<2;j++){let x=j?s2:s1;
    b.r(x-70,top+fh*.10,140,fh*.6,p.deep,.55);b.r(x-62,top+fh*.14,124,fh*.52,0x556d77,.62);
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let xx=x-53+i*34;b.r(xx,top+fh*.16,22,fh*.36,i%2?0x576c70:0x6a7c79,.67);b.r(xx+4,top+fh*.17,3,fh*.2,p.shine,.25)}
    ornament(b,x,top+fh*.39,'gear',p,1.4);
   }
   for(let x of [cx-20,cx+20]){b.r(x,top+fh*.04,5,fh*.87,p.trim,.7);b.r(x+1,top+fh*.04,2,fh*.87,p.shine,.5)}
   for(let y=top+fh*.12;y<bot-12;y+=20)b.r(cx-13,y,26,5,0x8e8882,.8);
   for(let i=0;i<7;i++){let x=33+i*(W-66)/6;b.r(x,25,10,73,p.deep,.81);b.r(x+3,28,4,66,p.trim,.54)}
  }else if(id==='opera'||p.id==='royal'){
   // Aristocratic fan-shaped procession, outer arc and framed stage, not a room-wide wallpaper.
   b.r(W*.24,96,W*.52,52,0x4a263d,.9);b.r(W*.25,98,W*.50,9,p.trim,.75);
   for(let k=0;k<18;k++){let x=W*.26+k*W*.027;b.r(x,108,W*.018,36,k%2?0x8f435b:0xb4506c,.91)}
   for(let ring=0;ring<4;ring++){
    const yy=top+fh*.40+ring*21;
    for(let i=0;i<11+ring*2;i++){let a=Math.PI*.15+Math.PI*.70*i/(10+ring*2),rad=W*.22+ring*18;
     let x=cx+Math.cos(a)*rad,y=yy+Math.sin(a)*19;
     if(x>35&&x<W-35&&y<bot-38)b.e(x,y,14,8,0xd0a487,.32)}
   }
   for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let x=27+i*(W-54)/3;ornament(b,x,67,'column',p,1.05)}
   b.r(cx-35,top+fh*.17,70,fh*.68,0xa78566,.27);b.r(cx-19,top+fh*.17,38,fh*.68,p.trim,.14);
  }else if(id==='chronos'||p.id==='guild'){
   const mx=W*.35,rx=W*.71;
   b.r(26,top+15,W*.39,fh*.68,p.deep,.56);b.r(W*.56,top+15,W*.39,fh*.68,0x6d443b,.58);
   b.r(35,top+24,W*.35,4,p.trim,.67);b.r(W*.58,top+24,W*.34,4,p.trim,.67);
   for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let y=top+39+i*38;ornament(b,mx,y,'dial',p,.82+i*.05)}
   for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let x=W*.62+i*W*.078;ornament(b,x,top+fh*.46+(i%2)*21,'gear',p,.92)}
   // Curved inlay separating public showcase and private workbench.
   b.e(cx,top+fh*.52,77,75,p.deep,.92);b.e(cx,top+fh*.52,63,61,p.trim,.86);b.e(cx,top+fh*.52,50,50,0x735741,.9);
   for(let k=0;k<12;k++){let a=k*Math.PI/6;b.r(cx+Math.cos(a)*25-1,top+fh*.52+Math.sin(a)*25-1,3,3,p.shine,.72)}
   for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let x=26+i*(W-52)/4;ornament(b,x,60,'dial',p,.75)}
  }else if(p.id==='tech'){
   // Technological circulation spine, modular screen cells, glass partitions.
   for(let side of [0,1]){let x=side?W*.59:W*.08,w=W*.33;
    b.r(x,top+8,w,fh*.64,p.deep,.57);b.r(x+7,top+14,w-14,fh*.56,0x2d627b,.57);
    for(let k=0;k<3;k++){let yy=top+25+k*30;b.r(x+18,yy,w-36,13,k%2?0x59a6a8:0x34758d,.64);b.r(x+22,yy+4,Math.max(7,w-76),2,p.shine,.55)}}
   for(let k=0;k<9;k++){let yy=top+17+k*fh*.07;b.r(cx-10,yy,20,3,p.trim,.44)}
   for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const x=30+i*(W-60)/4;b.r(x,28,5,63,p.trim,.49);b.r(x+7,31,21,33,0x64bbbd,.28)}
  }else if(p.id==='art'){
   // Gallery: unequal diagonal galleries, painterly inlay without gridded noise.
   for(let k=0;k<4;k++){let x=25+k*(W-50)/4,w=92+((variant+k)%3)*21;
    b.tri(x,top+16,x+w,top+18,x+w-15,top+fh*.62,[0x9b698c,0x64879b,0xba8d8e][k%3],.43);
    b.l(x,top+16,x+w-15,top+fh*.62,p.shine,.36,3)}
   b.r(cx-24,top+fh*.26,48,fh*.57,p.deep,.28);
   for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let x=23+i*(W-46)/4;b.r(x,28,35,43,p.deep,.91);b.r(x+4,32,27,35,[0xa275a7,0x86bca9,0xd9a37f][i%3],.91)}
  }else if(p.id==='heritage'||p.id==='forest'){
   for(let k=0;k<5;k++){let x=24+k*(W-48)/4,w=43+((variant+k)%2)*14;
    b.r(x,top+17,w,fh*.48,0x977554,.33);b.l(x,top+17,x+w*.35,top+fh*.52,0xdabb7a,.29,4)}
   b.e(W*.32,top+fh*.49,120,72,0x967249,.47);b.e(W*.75,top+fh*.55,103,63,0x5d5038,.49);
   for(let k=0;k<5;k++){let x=32+k*(W-64)/4;b.l(x,19,x+20+variant*3,91,p.deep,.73,6)}
  }else{
   // Civic: street-facing facade with two asymmetrical private/public bays.
   for(let k=0;k<3;k++){let x=30+k*(W-60)/3,w=k===variant%3?W*.23:W*.16;
    b.r(x,top+22,w,fh*.46,k%2?0x65847a:0x977f5f,.39);b.r(x+6,top+28,w-12,5,p.trim,.44)}
   for(let k=0;k<4;k++){let x=32+k*(W-64)/3;b.r(x,31,35,55,p.deep,.83);b.r(x+4,34,27,47,0x7ea6a2,.51);b.l(x+17,34,x+17,80,p.trim,.45,3)}
  }
  // Occupation-driven composition: meaningful zones, not random decoration.
  // Visible variation corresponds to the motif used in the uniqueness inspector.
  if(!m.signature){
   const handed=m.motif%2?-1:1,sideX=handed>0?W*.74:W*.26,backY=top+fh*.24;
   if(role==='home'){
     b.e(sideX,top+fh*.50,W*.25,fh*.30,p.trim,.21);
     b.e(sideX,top+fh*.50,W*.18,fh*.20,p.deep,.31);
     b.r(sideX-17,top+fh*.46,34,6,p.shine,.24);
   }else if(role==='trade'){
     b.r(sideX-W*.13,top+fh*.12,W*.26,fh*.59,p.deep,.17);
     for(let j=0;j<3;j++)b.r(sideX-W*.10,top+fh*.20+j*fh*.13,W*.20,4,p.trim,.33);
     b.l(sideX-W*.16,top+fh*.78,sideX+W*.16,top+fh*.78,p.trim,.38,3);
   }else if(role==='archive'){
     for(let j=0;j<4;j++){
       const x=sideX-W*.11+j*W*.07;
       b.r(x,top+fh*.14,5,fh*.56,p.deep,.30);
       b.r(x+2,top+fh*.16,2,fh*.48,p.trim,.36);
     }
   }else if(role==='kitchen'){
     b.e(sideX,top+fh*.44,W*.23,fh*.24,0xba7a50,.23);
     for(let j=0;j<6;j++)b.r(sideX-25+j*10,top+fh*.45-(j%2)*3,5,4,j%2?p.shine:p.trim,.54);
   }else if(role==='community'){
     b.e(sideX,top+fh*.50,W*.23,fh*.28,p.trim,.22);
     b.r(sideX-25,top+fh*.50-4,50,8,p.deep,.29);
   }else if(role==='office'){
     for(let j=0;j<3;j++){let xx=sideX-35+j*26;b.r(xx,top+fh*.19,19,fh*.29,p.deep,.24);b.r(xx+4,top+fh*.23,11,3,p.shine,.46)}
   }
   // A few asymmetrically placed architectural seams, derived from a stable identity.
   for(let j=0;j<2;j++){
     const xf=26+(m.motif*19+j*103)%(Math.max(32,W-70));
     b.r(xf,26,3,50+j*13,p.trim,.23);
   }
  }
  // Quiet passage and contact band: visual only, does not alter collision or navmesh.
  b.r(cx-20,bot-64,40,66,p.deep,.10);
  b.r(20,bot-6,W-40,3,p.trim,.29);
  // Four corners have at most a few semantic props; no random tiling or clutter.
  for(let k=0;k<Math.min(3,m.safeZones.length);k++){let r=m.safeZones[k],mx=r.x+r.w*.5,my=r.y+r.h*.48;
   let prop=p.id==='roots'?'plant':p.id==='mine'?'gear':p.id==='guild'?'dial':p.id==='royal'?'column':p.id==='tech'?'lamp':p.id==='forest'?'plant':p.id==='art'?'lamp':'plant';
   ornament(b,mx,my,prop,p,.52);
  }
 }
 function drawItem(g,it,s){if(!prefs.enabled||!it||!Number.isFinite(it.x))return;let m=plan(s);if(!m)return;
  const b=gfx(g),p=m.p,kind=String(it.k||''),x=it.x*T,y=it.y*T,w=it.w*T,h=it.h*T;
  if(!Number.isFinite(w)||w<20||h<18)return;
  // Shading always fits within the original item footprint (no new hitboxes).
  if(/table|desk|workbench|counter/.test(kind)){
   b.r(x+7,y+7,w-14,4,p.trim,.46);b.l(x+9,y+h-8,x+w-9,y+h-8,p.deep,.62,2);
   if(m.id==='chronos')for(let j=0;j<Math.min(3,Math.floor(w/32));j++)ornament(b,x+20+j*23,y+17,'dial',p,.45);
   if(m.id==='mine'){for(let j=0;j<4&&j*14<w-23;j++)b.r(x+12+j*14,y+15,9,6,j%2?p.trim:p.shine,.63)}
  }else if(/shelf|rack|cabinet|display/.test(kind)){
   for(let j=0;j<Math.min(5,Math.floor(w/19));j++){let xx=x+12+j*17;b.r(xx,y+13,5,Math.min(10,h-17),p.trim,.52);b.r(xx+7,y+13,3,Math.min(6,h-17),p.shine,.53)}
  }else if(/plant|herb/.test(kind))ornament(b,x+w*.5,y+h*.5,'plant',p,.5);
 }
 function drawFront(g,s){if(!prefs.enabled)return;const m=plan(s);if(!m)return;const b=gfx(g),{p,W,H:HH,id}=m;
  // Thin architectural edge only; never draws across central exit corridor.
  b.r(14,91,W-28,4,p.deep,.55);b.r(21,93,W-42,2,p.shine,.23);
  for(let x of [24,W-24]){
   ornament(b,x,83,id==='chronos'?'dial':id==='mine'?'gear':id==='opera'?'column':p.id==='roots'?'plant':'lamp',p,.54);
  }
  b.r(W/2-34,HH-43,68,2,p.trim,.28);
 }
 // Live exterior accents: semantic doorstep/travel marks in camera vicinity, decoration only.
 let outdoors=null,sceneRef=null,areaRef='',tick=0,stats={rendered:0,scanned:0,duplicates:0};
 function drawWorld(sc,dt){if(sc?.inside){outdoors?.clear();return}if(!sc?.area||!sc?.player||!prefs.enabled||!prefs.outdoors||!sc.add?.graphics)return;
  tick+=clamp(dt||16,0,150);if(tick<135)return;tick=0;
  const area=sc.area.id;if(sceneRef!==sc||areaRef!==area||!outdoors?.active){try{outdoors?.destroy()}catch{};outdoors=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(7.52);sceneRef=sc;areaRef=area}
  outdoors.clear();const b=gfx(outdoors),p=STYLE[area]||STYLE['english-town'],px=sc.player.x,py=sc.player.y,t=(sc.time?.now||0)/1000;
  let rendered=0;for(const building of (sc.area.buildings||[])){
   if(rendered>=5)break;const xx=(building.door+.5)*32,yy=(building.y+building.h+.6)*32;
   if(Math.abs(xx-px)>320||Math.abs(yy-py)>255)continue;
   // Do not draw over the doorway or interior entrance activation footprint.
   let sel=(hash(area+building.name)%4),shade=p.deep;
   for(let side of [-1,1]){let x=xx+side*(40+sel*2),y=yy+4;
    b.r(x-7,y,14,4,shade,.21);if(p.id==='roots'||p.id==='forest')ornament(b,x,y-3,'plant',p,.52);
    else if(p.id==='mine')b.r(x-3,y-13,6,13,p.trim,.3);
    else if(p.id==='royal')b.r(x-4,y-11,8,12,p.trim,.34);
    else if(p.id==='guild')b.r(x-9,y-7,18,5,p.trim,.43);
    else b.r(x-4,y-6,9,3,p.trim,.32);
   }
   // Real-world narrative footprint — used path, not random clutter.
   for(let z=0;z<2;z++)b.r(xx-6+z*11,yy+18+(z%2)*5,4,2,p.trim,.19);
   rendered++;
  }
  stats.rendered=rendered;
 }
 function onLeave(){/* Outdoor layer is reused across rooms; destroyed on scene change */}
 function onEnter(){/* Non-structural overlays never change entrance logic */}
 function compare(a,b){if(!a||!b)return 0;
  let partsA=a.footprint.split('|'),partsB=b.footprint.split('|');let score=0;
  for(let k=0;k<partsA.length;k++){score+=(partsA[k]===partsB[k]?[.34,.24,.13,.10,.10,.09][k]:0)}
  return Math.round(score*100);
 }
 function audit(){let arr=[...visits.values()],dupes=[],rooms=[];
  for(let i=0;i<arr.length;i++){
   let a=arr[i],top={score:0,key:''};for(let j=0;j<i;j++){let q=compare(a,arr[j]);if(q>top.score)top={score:q,key:arr[j].key}}
   let occupiedPixels=a.occupied.reduce((t,it)=>t+Math.max(0,it.w)*Math.max(0,it.h),0);
   const area=a.W*a.H,percent=Math.round(occupiedPixels/area*100);
   let notes=[];if(percent<5)notes.push('Mật độ vật thể thấp — xem lại công năng, không tự rải đồ');
   if(percent>32)notes.push('Vật thể dày — cần kiểm tra hành lang');
   if(top.score>=85)notes.push('Bố cục khá giống '+top.key+' — xem lại silhouette/mặt bằng');
   rooms.push({key:a.key,name:a.building,area:a.p.label,shape:a.shape,role:a.role,occupiedPercent:percent,nearest:top,notes});
   if(top.score>=85)dupes.push({a:a.key,b:top.key,score:top.score});
  }
  stats.scanned=arr.length;stats.duplicates=dupes.length;
  return {rooms,duplicates:dupes,stats:{...stats}};
 }

 }

  refresh();return p;
 }
 const save=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem('lq62_visual',JSON.stringify(prefs))}catch{}};

 if(typeof document!=='undefined'){

   if(e.code==='Escape'){let p=document.getElementById('lq62-panel');if(p&&!p.hidden){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();p.hidden=true}}
  },true);

 }

const hash=s=>{let h=2166136261;for(let i=0;i<String(s).length;i++)h=Math.imul(h^String(s).charCodeAt(i),16777619);return h>>>0};
const lim=(x,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const choose=(list,seed)=>list[(seed%list.length+list.length)%list.length];
const pict={
 family:['A shared hearth','A family collection','Woven household alcove'],
 work:['Trade bench','Tool station','Service counter'],
 learning:['Reference archive','Study alcove','Archive shelves'],
 cultural:['Ceremonial display','Communal gathering','Preserved records'],
 tech:['Data instruments','Interface consoles','Laboratory station']
};

try{const j=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('lq63_graphics')||'null');if(j&&typeof j==='object')options={...options,...j}}catch{}

const classify=s=>{const n=String(s?.__lq58?.building||'').toLowerCase(),region=config(s).kind;
 if(/vaela/.test(n))return 'vaela';if(/great mine|freight workshop/.test(n))return 'great-mine';
 if(/grand opera/.test(n))return 'grand-opera';if(/chronos|fine clocks/.test(n))return 'chronos';
 if(/council|town hall|elder|palace|royal court/.test(n))return 'council';
 if(/library|academy|archive|school|museum/.test(n))return 'learning';
 if(/bakery|baker|cafe|food|tavern|kitchen/.test(n))return 'food';
 if(/shop|market|guild|stall|jewel|cloth|exchange|bazaar/.test(n))return 'store';
 if(/house|home|cottage|hut|dwelling/.test(n))return 'home';
 if(/workshop|foundry|station|factory|lab|nexus|office/.test(n))return 'work';
 return region==='tech'?'work':region==='royal'?'council':'community';
};
const G=g=>({
 rect:(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0)g.fillStyle(c,a).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h))},
 circle:(x,y,r,c,a=1)=>{if(r>0)g.fillStyle(c,a).fillCircle(x,y,r)},
 oval:(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0)g.fillStyle(c,a).fillEllipse(x,y,w,h)},
 line:(x,y,X,Y,c,a=1,t=2)=>g.lineStyle(t,c,a).lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(X),Math.round(Y)),
 tri:(x,y,X,Y,U,V,c,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillTriangle(x,y,X,Y,U,V),
 ring:(x,y,r,c,a=1,t=2)=>{if(r>0)g.lineStyle(t,c,a).strokeCircle(x,y,r)}
});
const profileCache=new WeakMap();
function profile(s){if(!s?.__lq58)return null;
 const signature=[s.w,s.h,s.items?.length||0,s.__lq58.area,s.__lq58.building,s.__lq58.floor||0,s.__lq64Revision||0].join('::');
 const old=profileCache.get(s);
 if(old && old.signature===signature && old.items===s.items)return old.p;
 const area=s.__lq58.area||'english-town',name=s.__lq58.building||'Interior',level=s.__lq58.floor||0;
 const key=[area,name,level].join('::');const type=classify(s),seed=hash(key),W=s.w*TILE,H=s.h*TILE,region=config(s);
 const objects=(s.items||[]).filter(o=>Number.isFinite(o.x)&&Number.isFinite(o.y)&&Number.isFinite(o.w)&&Number.isFinite(o.h));
 const layout=window.LQVisualV62?.plan?.(s)||null;
 const dominant=type==='vaela'?'branch':type==='great-mine'?'gantry':type==='grand-opera'?'arch':type==='chronos'?'clock':
 region.kind==='organic'?'branch':region.kind==='industrial'?'gantry':region.kind==='royal'?'arch':region.kind==='merchant'?'arcade':
 region.kind==='tech'?'glass':region.kind==='studio'?'sawtooth':region.kind==='heritage'?'truss':'townhouse';
 const layoutVariant=seed%17,role=type==='food'?'work':type==='home'?'family':type==='learning'?'learning':type==='council'?'cultural':
 type==='store'?'work':region.kind==='tech'?'tech':'cultural';
 const zoneNames=pict[role]||pict.cultural;
 let paths=[];const doorX=W*.5,doorY=H-64;
 for(const item of objects){if(item.w<=0||item.h<=0)continue;
   paths.push({x:item.x*32,y:item.y*32,w:item.w*32,h:item.h*32,kind:item.k});}
 // Decorative inlays must not increase physical obstruction. Occupancy covers only existing items.
 const floorCenterX=W*(.42+(seed%5)*.04);
 const unique=[region.kind,dominant,type,layoutVariant,Math.round(W/64),Math.round(H/64),objects.length,
   Math.round(objects.reduce((a,o)=>a+o.x+o.w/2,0)/Math.max(1,objects.length))].join('|');
 const p={key,area,name,region,type,dominant,role,zoneNames,layoutVariant,seed,W,H,objects,paths,layout,doorX,doorY,floorCenterX,unique};
 rooms.set(key,p);profileCache.set(s,{signature,items:s.items,p});return p;
}
// Architectural strokes are composed in the 0..100 wall band; floor motifs are flat and passable.
function wallMark(d,x,y,k,p,scale=1){const {a,b,d:dark,glow}=p;
 if(k==='arched'){
  d.rect(x-17*scale,y-2*scale,34*scale,32*scale,dark,.90);
  d.oval(x,y+4*scale,28*scale,29*scale,b,.72);d.rect(x-13*scale,y+4*scale,26*scale,24*scale,b,.72);
  d.rect(x-9*scale,y+7*scale,18*scale,21*scale,dark,.85);d.oval(x,y+8*scale,18*scale,15*scale,dark,.85);
  d.line(x,y+5*scale,x,y+28*scale,glow,.65,2*scale);
 }else if(k==='leaf'){
  d.line(x,y+20*scale,x,y-6*scale,dark,.90,5*scale);
  d.oval(x-8*scale,y+1*scale,17*scale,13*scale,a,.86);d.oval(x+7*scale,y-4*scale,20*scale,16*scale,a,.88);
  d.oval(x-1*scale,y-9*scale,17*scale,10*scale,glow,.33);
 }else if(k==='dial'){
  d.circle(x,y,16*scale,dark,.86);d.circle(x,y,13*scale,b,.95);d.circle(x,y,9*scale,0x604832,.95);
  d.line(x,y,x+5*scale,y-4*scale,glow,1,2*scale);d.line(x,y,x-4*scale,y+1*scale,b,1,2*scale);
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const ang=i*Math.PI/4;d.rect(x+Math.cos(ang)*10*scale-1,y+Math.sin(ang)*10*scale-1,2,2,glow,.89)}
 }else if(k==='conduit'){
  d.rect(x-18*scale,y-14*scale,36*scale,29*scale,dark,.83);d.rect(x-14*scale,y-10*scale,28*scale,20*scale,a,.79);
  for(let z=0;z<3;z++)d.rect(x-10*scale,y-5*scale+z*6*scale,20*scale,2*scale,glow,z===1?.74:.42);
  d.circle(x+10*scale,y+12*scale,3*scale,b,1);
 }else if(k==='frame'){
  d.rect(x-16*scale,y-13*scale,32*scale,27*scale,dark,.98);d.rect(x-13*scale,y-10*scale,26*scale,21*scale,b,.88);
  d.rect(x-10*scale,y-7*scale,20*scale,15*scale,a,.79);d.line(x-10*scale,y+3*scale,x+10*scale,y-5*scale,glow,.6,2*scale);
 }else if(k==='loom'){
  d.rect(x-15*scale,y-11*scale,4*scale,26*scale,dark,.9);d.rect(x+11*scale,y-11*scale,4*scale,26*scale,dark,.9);
  d.line(x-12*scale,y-11*scale,x+12*scale,y-11*scale,b,.9,3*scale);d.line(x-11*scale,y+11*scale,x+11*scale,y+11*scale,b,.9,3*scale);
  for(let i=0;i<6;i++)d.line(x-10*scale+i*4*scale,y-8*scale,x-10*scale+i*4*scale,y+10*scale,i%2?glow:a,.8,1*scale);
 }
}
function emblem(p){return p.dominant==='branch'?'leaf':p.dominant==='gantry'?'conduit':p.dominant==='clock'||p.dominant==='arcade'?'dial':p.dominant==='glass'?'conduit':p.dominant==='truss'?'loom':'frame'}
function heroWalls(d,p){const W=p.W,cx=W/2,c=p.region;
 switch(p.dominant){
 case 'branch':{
  // Organic ceilings use curved silhouettes made from layered triangular root supports.
  for(let side of [-1,1]){let xx=side<0?30:W-30;
   d.line(xx,93,xx-side*28,34,0x3e5836,.99,15);d.line(xx,79,xx-side*56,45,0x69805b,.91,9);
   d.oval(xx-side*39,44,92,46,0x426c49,.92);d.oval(xx-side*47,34,58,37,0x7fa36b,.89);
   d.line(xx,90,cx+side*28,91,c.d,.65,4);
  }
  for(let j=0;j<5;j++)wallMark(d,45+j*(W-90)/4,64,'leaf',c,.78);
  break;}
 case 'gantry':{
  d.rect(16,16,W-32,20,c.d,.94);d.rect(22,20,W-44,5,c.b,.72);
  for(let j=0;j<6;j++){let x=23+j*(W-46)/5;
   d.rect(x,36,6,61,c.d,.84);d.rect(x+2,36,2,59,c.b,.74);
   if(j<5)d.line(x+6,39,x+(W-46)/5,73,c.a,.5,3);
  }
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++)wallMark(d,72+j*(W-144)/3,66,'conduit',c,.78);
  break;}
 case 'arch':{
  d.rect(17,13,W-34,15,c.d,.96);d.rect(20,17,W-40,5,c.b,.79);
  for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const x=35+j*(W-70)/4;
   d.rect(x-10,42,20,55,c.d,.9);d.rect(x-7,45,14,48,c.a,.84);d.rect(x-13,36,26,10,c.b,.85);
  }
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++)wallMark(d,77+j*(W-154)/3,64,'arched',c,.78);
  break;}
 case 'clock':case 'arcade':{
  for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let x=27+j*(W-54)/4;
   d.rect(x-10,18,20,75,c.d,.91);d.rect(x-7,21,14,70,c.a,.56);
   wallMark(d,x,56,p.dominant==='clock'?'dial':'arched',c,.75);
  }
  d.rect(24,91,W-48,4,c.b,.73);break;}
 case 'glass':{
  for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let x=24+j*(W-48)/4;d.rect(x,13,5,82,c.d,.87);d.rect(x+5,18,(W-48)/4-9,49,c.a,.35);
   d.line(x+6,69,x+(W-48)/4-3,69,c.glow,.67,2);}
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++)wallMark(d,80+j*(W-160)/3,80,'conduit',c,.64);break;}
 case 'sawtooth':{
  for(let j=0;j<6;j++){let x=23+j*(W-46)/5;d.tri(x,20,x+35,20,x+20,62,[0xa6869f,0x9d88b1,0xa98f85][j%3],.77);}
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++)wallMark(d,82+j*(W-164)/3,76,'frame',c,.85);break;}
 case 'truss':{
  for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let x=22+j*(W-44)/4;d.line(x,92,x+24,23,c.d,.75,8);d.line(x+2,85,x+25,28,c.b,.6,2);}
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++)wallMark(d,80+j*(W-160)/3,79,'loom',c,.78);break;}
 default:{
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++){let x=35+j*(W-70)/3;d.rect(x-13,25,26,67,c.d,.86);d.rect(x-9,30,18,54,c.a,.68);}
  wallMark(d,cx,66,'frame',c,1);break;}
 }
}
function floorStage(d,p){const W=p.W,H=p.H,c=p.region,cy=lim(H*.55,160,H-118),cx=p.floorCenterX;
 // Floor embellishment is passable and visually subordinate to the actual furniture and door.
 if(p.type==='vaela'||p.dominant==='branch'){
  const rx=Math.min(132,W*.29),ry=Math.min(80,(H-130)*.30);
  d.oval(cx,cy,rx*2+15,ry*2+15,c.d,.24);d.oval(cx,cy,rx*2,ry*2,0xa88755,.28);
  for(let k=0;k<11;k++){let th=k*Math.PI*2/11;d.line(cx+Math.cos(th)*33,cy+Math.sin(th)*21,cx+Math.cos(th)*rx,cy+Math.sin(th)*ry,c.b,.31,2)}
  d.ring(cx,cy,35,c.b,.24,3);
 }else if(p.dominant==='gantry'){
  for(const dx of [-22,22])d.rect(cx+dx,110,5,H-183,c.b,.34);
  for(let y=125;y<H-72;y+=25)d.rect(cx-16,y,38,3,c.a,.46);
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++){let yy=138+j*46;d.rect(42,yy,70,5,c.d,.43);d.rect(W-112,yy,70,5,c.d,.43)}
 }else if(p.dominant==='arch'){
  d.rect(cx-26,115,52,H-187,c.d,.34);d.rect(cx-13,114,26,H-186,0xb25e6d,.32);
  for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let yy=130+j*34;d.line(40,yy,Math.min(W-40,120),yy+14,c.b,.26,2);d.line(W-40,yy,W-120,yy+14,c.b,.26,2)}
 }else if(p.dominant==='clock'){
  d.ring(cx,cy,Math.min(W*.21,93),c.d,.30,7);d.ring(cx,cy,Math.min(W*.17,78),c.b,.28,2);
  for(let k=0;k<12;k++){const a=k*Math.PI/6;d.rect(cx+Math.sin(a)*70-2,cy-Math.cos(a)*70-2,4,4,c.b,.35)}
 }else if(p.dominant==='glass'){
  for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let yy=130+j*(H-210)/4;d.rect(cx-25,yy,50,4,c.glow,.24)}
  for(let dx of [-70,70])d.line(cx+dx,125,cx+dx,H-96,c.a,.33,3);
 }else if(p.dominant==='sawtooth'){
  for(let k=0;k<4;k++){let x=42+k*(W-84)/4;d.tri(x,127,x+70,137,x+33,H-128,[0x9e7c97,0x6d9b91,0xc1977e][k%3],.13)}
 }else if(p.dominant==='truss'){
  for(let k=0;k<5;k++){let y=129+k*(H-204)/4;d.line(40,y,W-40,y+12,c.b,.20,3)}
 }else{
  for(let k=0;k<3;k++){let y=148+k*41;d.rect(35,y,Math.min(72,W*.17),4,c.b,.19);d.rect(W-107,y,Math.min(72,W*.17),4,c.b,.19)}
 }
 // Leave the door lane quiet and unmarked to maintain entrance readability.
 d.line(W*.5-23,H-39,W*.5+23,H-39,c.b,.31,2);
}
// Secondary architectural language reflects owner profession and individual layout.
// All pieces are flat floor inlays or wall-mounted; they are not freestanding props.
function occupationIdentity(d,p){
 const W=p.W,H=p.H,c=p.region,v=p.layoutVariant;
 const flip=(v%2)?1:-1;
 const anchor=W*(flip>0?.75:.25),second=W-anchor;
 const upper=130+v%3*8;
 if(p.role==='family'){
   // Family rooms: conversational rugs, asymmetrical alcove seams, low wall textile.
   d.oval(anchor,upper+48,Math.min(93,W*.24),50,c.b,.16);
   d.oval(anchor,upper+48,Math.min(70,W*.18),35,c.d,.17);
   for(let k=0;k<5;k++)d.line(anchor-28+k*14,upper+26,anchor-20+k*13,upper+62,c.a,.26,2);
   wallMark(d,second,66,'loom',c,.57);
 }else if(p.role==='work'){
   // Shops and workshops: a visual flow lane points from production to display.
   const x1=flip>0?35:W-35,x2=flip>0?W*.37:W*.63;
   for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
    const xx=x1+(x2-x1)*(j+1)/4;
    d.rect(xx-13,upper+j*26,26,4,c.d,.33);
    d.rect(xx-8,upper+j*26+5,16,2,c.b,.35);
   }
   wallMark(d,anchor,70,p.region.kind==='merchant'?'dial':'conduit',c,.62);
 }else if(p.role==='learning'){
   // Study/archive: a vertical reference strip and a reading zone on the wall.
   for(let j=0;j<5;j++){
    const xx=anchor-30+j*13;
    d.rect(xx,39+(j%2)*5,8,47,c.d,.62);
    d.rect(xx+2,42+(j%2)*5,4,41,j%2?c.b:c.a,.45);
   }
   d.rect(second-32,upper,65,4,c.b,.25);
   wallMark(d,second,63,'frame',c,.62);
 }else if(p.role==='tech'){
   // Routing diagram is an inset on the floor, not an extra blocking console.
   for(let j=0;j<4;j++){
    const y=upper+j*19;
    d.line(anchor-28,y,anchor+18,y,c.glow,.26,2);
    d.circle(anchor+20,y,2,c.b,.42);
   }
   wallMark(d,second,66,'conduit',c,.7);
 }else{
   // Communal gathering places: radial floor inlay, ceremonial wall display.
   d.ring(anchor,upper+48,31,c.b,.24,3);
   for(let j=0;j<7;j++){
    const a=j*Math.PI*2/7;
    d.rect(anchor+Math.cos(a)*35-2,upper+48+Math.sin(a)*35-2,4,4,c.a,.39);
   }
   wallMark(d,second,66,p.dominant==='arch'?'arched':'frame',c,.64);
 }
 // Individual construction seams, derived from a stable building ID instead of clutter.
 for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
   const x=26+((v*41+j*103)%(Math.max(50,W-52)));
   d.rect(x,21,3,23+(v+j)%4*7,c.d,.25);
   d.rect(x+3,24,2,13+(v+j)%4*7,c.b,.31);
 }
}
function drawBack(g,s){if(!options.enabled||!s?.__lq58)return;const p=profile(s);if(!p||p.W<250||p.H<220)return;
 const d=G(g);heroWalls(d,p);floorStage(d,p);occupationIdentity(d,p);
 // Signature landmarks in the actual upper wall, intentionally different shapes.
 if(p.type==='vaela'){
   for(let j=0;j<3;j++)wallMark(d,p.W*.32+j*p.W*.18,67,'leaf',p.region,1.25);
 }else if(p.type==='great-mine'){
   d.rect(p.W*.41,13,p.W*.18,19,0x172a35,.96);d.rect(p.W*.43,17,p.W*.14,9,p.region.glow,.74);
 }else if(p.type==='grand-opera'){
   d.rect(p.W*.29,16,p.W*.42,21,0x6e3451,.95);d.rect(p.W*.32,19,p.W*.36,7,p.region.b,.76);
 }else if(p.type==='chronos'){
   wallMark(d,p.W/2,40,'dial',p.region,1.45);
 }
}
function objectDetail(g,it,s){if(!options.enabled||!it?.k)return;const p=profile(s);if(!p)return;
 const x=it.x*TILE,y=it.y*TILE,w=it.w*TILE,h=it.h*TILE,k=String(it.k||'').toLowerCase(),d=G(g),c=p.region;
 if(!Number.isFinite(x+y+w+h)||w<16||h<16)return;
 const xmin=x+3,xmax=x+w-3,ymin=y+3,ymax=y+h-3;
 // Every line is clipped conceptually to the real object's own bounding box.
 if(/shelf|cabinet|book|display|rack/.test(k)){
   for(let j=0;j<Math.max(1,Math.min(5,Math.floor((w-10)/17)));j++){
      const xx=xmin+4+j*Math.max(10,(w-15)/Math.max(1,Math.min(5,Math.floor((w-10)/17))));
      if(xx+5>xmax)break;
      d.rect(xx,ymin+6,3,Math.min(17,h-12),j%2?c.a:c.b,.86);
      if(h>33)d.rect(xx+1,ymax-7,4,3,c.glow,.53);
   }
 }else if(/desk|table|bench|counter|stove|forge|furnace|machine/.test(k)){
   const lineY=ymin+Math.min(10,(h-6)*.28);
   d.line(xmin+6,lineY,xmax-6,lineY,c.b,.62,2);
   for(let j=0;j<Math.min(4,Math.floor(w/21));j++){
      const xx=xmin+11+j*21;
      if(xx+4<xmax&&lineY+9<ymax){d.rect(xx,lineY+5,6,5,j%2?c.glow:c.d,.76);d.rect(xx+1,lineY+6,2,2,c.b,.82)}
   }
   if(p.type==='chronos'||p.region.kind==='merchant'){
      const cx=x+w*.71,cy=y+h*.53;let rr=Math.min(11,w*.11,h*.25);
      if(rr>3){d.circle(cx,cy,rr,c.b,.83);d.circle(cx,cy,rr*.7,c.d,.81);d.line(cx,cy,cx+rr*.4,cy-rr*.3,c.glow,.86,2)}
   }
 }else if(/bed|sofa|chair|seat/.test(k)){
   d.rect(xmin+4,ymin+4,Math.max(4,w-15),Math.min(6,h-9),c.b,.59);
   if(h>28)d.rect(xmin+8,ymax-6,Math.max(4,w-23),2,c.d,.51);
 }else if(/plant|herb|tree/.test(k)){
   const mid=x+w*.5,top=y+h*.31;
   d.line(mid,top+5,mid,ymax-7,c.d,.76,2);
   d.oval(mid-5,top,Math.min(9,w*.25),Math.min(7,h*.2),c.a,.82);
   d.oval(mid+5,top-4,Math.min(9,w*.25),Math.min(7,h*.2),c.glow,.63);
 }
}
function drawFront(g,s){if(!options.enabled||!s?.__lq58)return;const p=profile(s);if(!p)return;const d=G(g),W=p.W,H=p.H,c=p.region;
 // Wall cornice and entrance threshold live on the texture, don't block walking.
 d.rect(14,94,W-28,3,c.d,.8);d.rect(20,96,W-40,2,c.b,.42);
 for(let side of [-1,1]){let x=side<0?22:W-22;d.rect(x-4,72,8,26,c.d,.82);d.rect(x-1,75,2,18,c.b,.60)}
 // Personality: meaningful small wall badges, not tiled floor clutter.
 const motif=emblem(p);wallMark(d,54,88,motif,c,.53);wallMark(d,W-54,88,motif,c,.53);
}
function insideAmbient(sc,elapsed){if(!sc?.inside?.spec||!options.enabled||!options.ambient)return;
 const ins=sc.inside,s=ins.spec,p=profile(s),key=p?.key;if(!p)return;
 if(sceneRef!==sc||insideKey!==key||!insideGraphics?.active){
   if(insideGraphics){try{insideGraphics.destroy()}catch{}}
   insideGraphics=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(1.35);ins.objs.push(insideGraphics);sceneRef=sc;insideKey=key;
 }
 frame+=elapsed||16;if(frame<110)return;frame=0;
 const d=G(insideGraphics),clock=(sc.time?.now||0)/1000,scale=TILE*ins.k,baseX=ins.objs[0].x,baseY=ins.objs[0].y,c=p.region;
 insideGraphics.clear();const gameMinutes=Number(sc.store?.game?.minutes??720),night=gameMinutes<400||gameMinutes>1130;
 let n=0;for(const it of p.objects){if(n>=8)break;
  const k=String(it.k||'').toLowerCase(),ix=baseX+(it.x+it.w*.5)*scale,iy=baseY+(it.y+.5)*scale;
  if(!Number.isFinite(ix+iy))continue;
  if(/lamp|candle|torch|fire|forge|furnace|stove|crystal/.test(k)){
    const r=4+Math.sin(clock*3+n)*1.2;
    d.circle(ix,iy,Math.max(2,r*ins.k),/crystal/.test(k)?0x9ceaf5:0xffd48c,night?.37:.18);
    d.circle(ix,iy,Math.max(1,2*ins.k),c.glow,.60);n++;
  }else if(/clock|gear|machine|engine|meter/.test(k)){
    const r=Math.max(5,6*ins.k),xx=ix+Math.cos(clock*.8+n)*r*.5,yy=iy+Math.sin(clock*.8+n)*r*.5;
    d.line(ix,iy,xx,yy,c.glow,.54,Math.max(1,ins.k));n++;
  }else if(/plant|herb|tree|vase/.test(k)){
    const wind=Math.sin(clock*1.3+n)*Math.min(4,ins.k*2.0);
    d.line(ix,iy+2*ins.k,ix+wind,iy-7*ins.k,c.a,.55,2);n++;
  }
 }
}
function outsideAmbient(sc,elapsed){if(!sc?.area||sc.inside||!options.outdoor||!options.enabled)return;
 if(outsideScene!==sc||!outsideGraphics?.active){
  try{outsideGraphics?.destroy()}catch{}
  outsideGraphics=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(6.6);outsideScene=sc;
 }
 frame+=elapsed||16;if(frame<200)return;frame=0;
 outsideGraphics.clear();let count=0;
 const d=G(outsideGraphics),c=REGIONS[sc.area.id]||REGIONS['english-town'],clock=(sc.time?.now||0)/1000;
 const buildingList=(sc.area.buildings||[]).map(b=>({b,x:(b.door+.5)*TILE,y:(b.y+.15)*TILE}))
  .filter(q=>Math.abs(q.x-sc.player.x)<530&&Math.abs(q.y-sc.player.y)<400)
  .sort((a,b)=>(a.x-sc.player.x)**2+(a.y-sc.player.y)**2-(b.x-sc.player.x)**2-(b.y-sc.player.y)**2).slice(0,7);
 for(const entry of buildingList){
  const {b,x,y}=entry;
  // Only on the existing upper facade, never inside walkway or door trigger.
  const cx=x+((hash(sc.area.id+b.name)%3)-1)*11;
  const t=Math.sin(clock*1.2+count)*2;
  if(c.kind==='industrial'){
    d.rect(cx-2,y-2,4,16,c.b,.48);d.rect(cx+8,y+5+t,2,10,c.glow,.56);
  }else if(c.kind==='organic'||c.kind==='heritage'){
    d.line(cx-9,y+4,cx-2+t,y-11,c.d,.43,3);d.oval(cx-3+t,y-12,9,5,c.a,.60);
  }else if(c.kind==='royal'||c.kind==='merchant'){
    d.rect(cx-4,y-7,9,13,c.b,.40);d.rect(cx-2,y-4,5,6,c.glow,.22+.10*(Math.sin(clock*2)+1));
  }else if(c.kind==='tech'){
    d.rect(cx-8,y-6,16,7,c.a,.25);d.rect(cx-7,y-3,5+(Math.floor(clock*2)%8),2,c.glow,.58);
  }else if(c.kind==='studio'){
    d.line(cx-7,y+3,cx+7,y-6,c.a,.34,2);d.rect(cx+2,y-2,4,4,c.glow,.37);
  }else{
    d.rect(cx-4,y-7,8,14,c.a,.31);d.rect(cx-1,y-5,2,9,c.glow,.31);
  }
  count++;
 }
}
function update(sc,dt){if(sc?.inside){outsideGraphics?.clear();insideAmbient(sc,dt)}else{insideGraphics?.clear();outsideAmbient(sc,dt)}}

function audit(){const all=[...rooms.values()],issues=[],families={},byMap={};let occupied=0;
 for(const p of all){families[p.dominant]=(families[p.dominant]||0)+1;byMap[p.area]=(byMap[p.area]||0)+1;
  const sum=p.paths.reduce((a,o)=>a+o.w*o.h,0),ratio=sum/(p.W*p.H);occupied+=ratio;
  if(ratio>.38)issues.push({room:p.key,type:'crowded',score:Math.round(ratio*100)});
  if(ratio<.035)issues.push({room:p.key,type:'sparse',score:Math.round(ratio*100)});
 }
 const near=[];
 for(let i=0;i<all.length;i++)for(let j=0;j<i;j++){
  const a=all[i],b=all[j];if(a.area!==b.area)continue;
  const matches=['dominant','type','layoutVariant','role'].reduce((n,k)=>n+(a[k]===b[k]),0);
  if(matches===4&&Math.abs(a.W-b.W)<33&&Math.abs(a.H-b.H)<33)near.push([a.key,b.key]);
 }
 return {version:VERSION,rooms:all.length,families,byMap,nearMatches:near.slice(0,25),issues,avgFurniturePercent:Math.round(occupied/Math.max(1,all.length)*100)};
}

function toggle(){let p=mount();if(p)p.hidden=!p.hidden}
if(typeof document!=='undefined'){
 window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.repeat||['INPUT','TEXTAREA','SELECT'].includes(String(e.target?.tagName||'')))return;

 },true);
 let check=0;const link=()=>{const root=document.getElementById('l11-page')||document.getElementById('l11-dialog');if(root&&!root.querySelector('#lq63-launch')){
  const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='lq63-launch';b.type='button';b.textContent='◇ V63 · Đồ họa thủ công';b.style.cssText='margin:6px;padding:9px 13px;border-radius:9px;border:1px solid #9aafaa;background:#294b52;color:#edf6ee;cursor:pointer';b.onclick=toggle;root.appendChild(b);
 }};

}

/* LIFEQUEST AI V64 — VISUAL INTELLIGENCE
   Built for V63. Additional overlays do not change gameplay collision polygons.
   Diagnostics inspect existing geometry; changes to navigation use the existing physics module.
*/
const LQ64 = (() => {
 'use strict';
 const VERSION = '64.0.0', TILE = 32;
 const storageKey='lifequest_v64_visual';
 const defaults={enabled:true,ambient:true,quality:'balanced',debug:false};
 let opts={...defaults};
 try { const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(storageKey)||'null');if(v&&typeof v==='object')opts={...opts,...v}; } catch(_){}
 const save=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(storageKey,JSON.stringify(opts))}catch(_){}};
 const clamp=(x,lo,hi)=>Math.max(lo,Math.min(hi,x));
 const hash=s=>{let h=2166136261; for(const ch of String(s))h=Math.imul(h^ch.charCodeAt(0),16777619);return h>>>0;};
 let roomStore=new Map(), scene=null, insideDraw=null, worldDraw=null, debugDraw=null;
 let roomKey='', clockMs=0, debugMs=0, ambientStats={objects:0,lastMs:0}, ui;
 const r=(g,x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>{if(w<=0||h<=0)return;g.fillStyle(c,a).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h));};
 const line=(g,x,y,u,v,c,a=1,width=1)=>g.lineStyle(width,c,a).lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(u),Math.round(v));
 const circ=(g,x,y,R,c,a=1)=>{if(R<=0)return;g.fillStyle(c,a).fillCircle(x,y,R)};
 const ell=(g,x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>{if(w<=0||h<=0)return;g.fillStyle(c,a).fillEllipse(x,y,w,h)};
 const ring=(g,x,y,R,c,a=.7,width=2)=>{if(R<=0)return;g.lineStyle(width,c,a).strokeCircle(x,y,R)};
 function collect(s){if(!s?.__lq58)return null;let p=window.LQVisualV63?.profile?.(s);if(p){roomStore.set(p.key,p);}return p||null;}
 const signed=(v,a=1)=>Math.round(v*a);
 function back(g,s){
  if(!opts.enabled)return;let p=collect(s);if(!p)return;
  const W=p.W,H=p.H,t=p.type,reg=p.region,seed=p.seed;
  if(W<210||H<180)return;
  const dark=reg.d,acc=reg.a,trim=reg.b,glow=reg.glow;
  const wallTop=15,wallBottom=93;
  // Themed architectural gestures stay in the upper wall band; floor inlays are passable.
  if(t==='vaela'||reg.kind==='organic'){
    // Curved roots frame the hearth, anchored to the existing back wall.
    for(let side of [-1,1]){
      let x=W/2+side*W*.28;
      for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
        let y=86-i*10, drift=side*(4+3*Math.sin(i*.58));
        line(g,x+drift,y,x+drift-side*8,y-11,dark,.78,4);
        line(g,x+drift-side*3,y-2,x+drift-side*5,y-7,trim,.56,1);
      }
      ell(g,x,65,22,12,acc,.66);ell(g,x+side*12,45,27,14,acc,.49);
    }
    ell(g,W/2,154,135,61,dark,.13);ring(g,W/2,154,45,trim,.41,3);ring(g,W/2,154,23,acc,.32,2);
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
      const a=i*Math.PI*2/9;
      line(g,W/2+Math.cos(a)*25,154+Math.sin(a)*13,W/2+Math.cos(a)*43,154+Math.sin(a)*23,trim,.30,1);
    }
  } else if(t==='great-mine'||reg.kind==='industrial'){
    // Gantry frames are visually tall, but do not create phantom colliders.
    for(const x of [W*.17,W*.5,W*.83]){
      r(g,x-9,18,18,75,dark,.69);r(g,x-5,22,10,64,acc,.65);
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++)r(g,x-2,24+i*12,4,4,trim,.65);
    }
    r(g,22,11,W-44,8,dark,.77);r(g,22,13,W-44,2,trim,.58);
    const x=W/2-28;
    r(g,x,33,56,46,dark,.93);r(g,x+5,39,46,28,acc,.68);
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++)r(g,x+9+i*10,46,5,12,i%2?trim:glow,.48);
    // Transport markings; these are paint, not tracks with gameplay collision.
    for(let side of [-1,1]){
      const xx=W/2+side*18;
      line(g,xx,109,xx+side*21,H-40,trim,.32,3);
      for(let y=135;y<H-35;y+=28){let at=xx+side*(y-109)*21/(H-149);r(g,at-side*8,y,16,3,dark,.20)}
    }
  } else if(t==='grand-opera'||reg.kind==='royal'){
    // Main proscenium, orchestra curve and visual aisle.
    const left=W*.21,right=W*.79;
    r(g,left,17,right-left,77,dark,.78);r(g,left+9,21,right-left-18,59,0x743449,.75);
    r(g,left+18,25,right-left-36,9,trim,.58);
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
      let xx=left+i*(right-left)/4;
      r(g,xx-6,12,12,83,dark,.91);r(g,xx-3,19,6,64,trim,.67);
    }
    const cx=W/2, cy=H*.60;
    for(let k=0;k<5;k++){
      const ry=27+k*17,rx=78+k*34;
      for(let i=0;i<32;i++){
        let a=Math.PI*.12+(Math.PI*.76*i/31),b=Math.PI*.12+(Math.PI*.76*(i+1)/31);
        line(g,cx-Math.cos(a)*rx,cy+Math.sin(a)*ry,cx-Math.cos(b)*rx,cy+Math.sin(b)*ry,trim,.13,1);
      }
    }
    r(g,W/2-22,98,44,H-125,0x8c3c4b,.19);
  } else if(t==='chronos'||(reg.kind==='merchant'&&/clock/i.test(p.name))){
    // Horological gallery: large numbered dial in wall, tools remain on real item footprints.
    const cx=W/2,cy=56,R=34;
    circ(g,cx,cy,R+5,dark,.96);circ(g,cx,cy,R,trim,.92);circ(g,cx,cy,R-6,dark,.93);
    for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
      let a=i*Math.PI/6,rr=R-10;
      r(g,cx+Math.sin(a)*rr-2,cy-Math.cos(a)*rr-2,4,4,glow,.84);
    }
    line(g,cx,cy,cx+12,cy-11,trim,.95,3);line(g,cx,cy,cx-2,cy-17,glow,.94,2);
    for(let side of [-1,1]){
      const x=cx+side*W*.30;
      r(g,x-21,31,42,54,dark,.79);r(g,x-17,36,34,43,acc,.7);
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ring(g,x,42+i*15,5,trim,.68,2);circ(g,x,42+i*15,2,glow,.63)}
    }
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++)ring(g,W/2,H*.56,27+i*26,trim,.10,2);
  } else if(reg.kind==='tech'){
    r(g,27,21,W-54,54,dark,.73);
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
      let x=40+i*(W-80)/4;
      r(g,x-8,28,16,36,acc,.67);r(g,x-5,33,10,18,glow,.32);
      line(g,x,70,x,85,trim,.53,2);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++)line(g,40+i*38,112,W-40-i*12,112+i*17,glow,.17,1);
  } else if(reg.kind==='studio'){
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      let x=36+i*(W-72)/4,y=27+i%2*13;
      r(g,x,y,26,37,dark,.79);r(g,x+3,y+3,20,31,[0xb39a69,0x6a93ae,0xca7796,0x7fae91][i],.63);
      line(g,x+7,y+30,x+20,y+12,trim,.46,2);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++)line(g,70+i*40,110,W*.55+i*24,H*.62,trim,.11,2);
  } else if(reg.kind==='heritage'){
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
      let x=28+i*(W-56)/5;
      line(g,x,21,x,92,dark,.69,5);line(g,x+2,26,x+2,88,trim,.44,1);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let x=54+i*(W-108)/4;line(g,x,23,x+12,10,acc,.51,2);line(g,x+12,10,x+24,23,acc,.51,2)}
  } else {
    // Civic rooms are anchored by windows with shadowed recesses, never a generic tiled overlay.
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      let x=40+i*(W-80)/3;
      r(g,x-15,25,30,48,dark,.71);r(g,x-10,28,20,39,acc,.54);
      r(g,x-2,30,4,35,trim,.64);r(g,x-10,46,20,3,trim,.51);
    }
  }
  // Per-room material strokes, sparse and seeded; skip the central entrance corridor.
  const count=opts.quality==='performance'?8:opts.quality==='cinematic'?21:13;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const seed2=hash(p.key+':'+i),xx=24+seed2%(W-48),yy=112+Math.floor(seed2/71)%Math.max(1,H-150);
    if(Math.abs(xx-W/2)<31 && yy>H*.66)continue;
    r(g,xx,yy,(seed2%3)+2,2,i%3?acc:dark,.12);
  }
 }
 function item(g,it,s){
  if(!opts.enabled||!it?.k)return; const p=collect(s);if(!p)return;
  const kind=String(it.k).toLowerCase(),x=it.x*TILE,y=it.y*TILE,w=it.w*TILE,h=it.h*TILE,c=p.region;
  if(!Number.isFinite(x+y+w+h)||w<22||h<22)return;
  const inner=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Math.round(Math.min(w,h)/15)));
  // Every ink stroke is inside the object's real footprint — never draws false solid objects in the aisle.
  if(/desk|table|bench|counter|anvil|forge|furnace|machine|engine/.test(kind)){
    r(g,x+5,y+5,w-10,3,c.b,.55);r(g,x+7,y+10,w-14,2,c.d,.65);
    const n=Math.min(4,Math.floor((w-12)/17));
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const xx=x+10+i*(w-18)/Math.max(1,n);
      r(g,xx,y+Math.min(h-13,16),6,4,i%2?c.glow:c.a,.82);
      r(g,xx+1,y+Math.min(h-12,17),2,2,c.d,.83);
    }
    if(/machine|engine|forge/.test(kind)){circ(g,x+w*.72,y+h*.59,Math.min(11,h*.23),c.d,.78);ring(g,x+w*.72,y+h*.59,Math.min(8,h*.17),c.b,.76,2)}
  }else if(/shelf|rack|cabinet|display|books/.test(kind)){
    const rows=Math.min(3,Math.floor(h/17));
    for(let i=0;i<rows;i++){
      const yy=y+7+i*(h-11)/Math.max(1,rows);
      r(g,x+5,yy,w-10,3,c.b,.57);
      for(let j=0;j<Math.min(7,Math.floor(w/10));j++){
        let xx=x+8+j*9;
        if(xx+6<x+w-5)r(g,xx,yy-6,5,6,j%2?c.a:c.glow,.47);
      }
    }
  }else if(/bed|sofa/.test(kind)){
    r(g,x+5,y+6,w-10,Math.min(11,h-12),c.b,.44);
    r(g,x+9,y+8,Math.min(17,w-18),Math.min(8,h-16),c.glow,.60);
    line(g,x+9,y+h-9,x+w-9,y+h-9,c.d,.62,2);
  }else if(/plant|herb|flower|tree|bush/.test(kind)){
    const cx=x+w/2,cy=y+h/2;
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const sign=i-1;
      line(g,cx,cy+8,cx+sign*8,cy-7-i*2,c.d,.65,2);
      ell(g,cx+sign*8,cy-7-i*2,Math.min(11,w*.33),Math.min(7,h*.23),c.a,.69);
    }
  }else if(/clock|gear|dial/.test(kind)){
    const cx=x+w*.5,cy=y+h*.5,R=Math.min(12,w*.23,h*.30);
    ring(g,cx,cy,R,c.b,.80,2);circ(g,cx,cy,2,c.glow,.93);line(g,cx,cy,cx+R*.4,cy-R*.45,c.glow,.79,2);
  }
 }
 function front(g,s){if(!opts.enabled)return;const p=collect(s);if(!p)return;
   const W=p.W,H=p.H,c=p.region;
   r(g,24,97,W-48,2,c.b,.44);
   // Low contrast edge around actual doorway, keeping the entrance visibly walkable.
   r(g,W/2-27,H-20,54,3,c.b,.30);
   for(let i=0;i<3;i++){r(g,W/2-27+i*23,H-17,5,2,c.d,.27)}
 }
 function unionArea(rects,W,H){
   const a=rects.map(q=>({x:clamp(q.x,0,W),r:clamp(q.x+q.w,0,W),y:clamp(q.y,0,H),b:clamp(q.y+q.h,0,H)})).filter(q=>q.r>q.x&&q.b>q.y);
   if(!a.length)return 0;
   const X=[...new Set(a.flatMap(q=>[q.x,q.r]))].sort((u,v)=>u-v);
   let total=0;
   for(let i=0;i<X.length-1;i++){
     let segments=a.filter(q=>q.x<X[i+1]&&q.r>X[i]).map(q=>[q.y,q.b]).sort((u,v)=>u[0]-v[0]);
     let lo=-1,hi=-1,covered=0;
     for(const [u,v] of segments){if(lo<0){lo=u;hi=v;}else if(u>hi){covered+=hi-lo;lo=u;hi=v;}else hi=Math.max(hi,v)}
     if(lo>=0)covered+=hi-lo;
     total+=(X[i+1]-X[i])*covered;
   }
   return total;
 }
 function occupancyGrid(p,c=12,rw=9){
   let bits=new Uint8Array(c*rw);
   for(let row=0;row<rw;row++)for(let col=0;col<c;col++){
     let x0=p.W*col/c,y0=p.H*row/rw,x1=p.W*(col+1)/c,y1=p.H*(row+1)/rw;
     const q=p.paths||[];
     bits[row*c+col]=q.some(k=>Math.min(k.x+k.w,x1)-Math.max(k.x,x0)>0.2*(x1-x0)&&Math.min(k.y+k.h,y1)-Math.max(k.y,y0)>0.2*(y1-y0))?1:0;
   }
   return bits;
 }
 function similarity(a,b){
   if(!a||!b||a.area!==b.area)return 0;
   const ga=occupancyGrid(a),gb=occupancyGrid(b);let intersection=0,union=0;
   for(let i=0;i<ga.length;i++){if(ga[i]&&gb[i])intersection++;if(ga[i]||gb[i])union++;}
   const spatial=union?intersection/union:0;
   const size=Math.max(0,1-(Math.abs(a.W-b.W)+Math.abs(a.H-b.H))/(Math.max(a.W,b.W)+Math.max(a.H,b.H)));
   const typ=a.type===b.type?1:0,dom=a.dominant===b.dominant?1:0;
   return Math.round(100*(spatial*.58+size*.24+typ*.12+dom*.06));
 }
 function report(){
   const rooms=[...roomStore.values()],issues=[],similar=[],byMap={};
   for(const p of rooms){
     byMap[p.area]=(byMap[p.area]||0)+1;
     const occupied=unionArea(p.paths,p.W,p.H),ratio=occupied/(p.W*p.H);
     const doorClear=p.paths.every(q=>!(q.x<p.W/2+24&&q.x+q.w>p.W/2-24&&q.y+q.h>p.H-60));
     if(ratio>.42)issues.push({room:p.key,kind:'dense',coverage:Math.round(ratio*100)});
     if(ratio<.035)issues.push({room:p.key,kind:'sparse',coverage:Math.round(ratio*100)});
     if(!doorClear)issues.push({room:p.key,kind:'door-review',coverage:Math.round(ratio*100)});
   }
   for(let i=0;i<rooms.length;i++)for(let j=0;j<i;j++){
     if(rooms[i].area!==rooms[j].area)continue;
     const score=similarity(rooms[i],rooms[j]);
     if(score>=86)similar.push({a:rooms[i].key,b:rooms[j].key,score});
   }
   similar.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
   return {version:VERSION,observedRooms:rooms.length,byMap,similar:similar.slice(0,50),totalSimilar:similar.length,issues,physicsIndex:window.LQPrecisePhysics?.indexStats?.()||null,render:ambientStats,settings:{...opts}};
 }
 function drawAmbient(sc,dt){
   if(!sc||!opts.enabled||!opts.ambient)return;
   clockMs+=(Number.isFinite(dt)?dt:16);const wait=opts.quality==='performance'?300:opts.quality==='cinematic'?100:160;
   if(clockMs<wait)return;clockMs=0;
   if(sc.inside?.spec){
     const ins=sc.inside,p=collect(ins.spec);if(!p)return;
     if(!insideDraw?.active||scene!==sc||roomKey!==p.key){
       try{insideDraw?.destroy()}catch(_){}
       insideDraw=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(1.33);ins.objs.push(insideDraw);scene=sc;roomKey=p.key;
     }
     insideDraw.clear();const gr=insideDraw,k=ins.k,origin=ins.objs[0];
     if(!origin||!Number.isFinite(origin.x+origin.y))return;
     const now=(sc.time?.now||0)/1000,xx=origin.x+p.W*k*.5, yy=origin.y+46*k;
     const gl=p.region.glow;
     if(p.type==='chronos'){const R=24*k;line(gr,xx,yy,xx+Math.cos(now*.7)*R,yy+Math.sin(now*.7)*R,gl,.55,Math.max(1,k));}
     else if(p.type==='great-mine'){for(let i=0;i<3;i++)r(gr,xx-17*k+i*14*k,yy+5*k,8*k,4*k,gl,.24+.21*Math.sin(now*2+i)**2)}
     else if(p.type==='grand-opera'){ell(gr,xx,yy,40*k,8*k,gl,.13+.06*Math.sin(now*1.6));}
     else if(p.type==='vaela'){
        line(gr,xx-25*k,yy,xx-23*k+3*k*Math.sin(now),yy-19*k,p.region.a,.42,2);
        circ(gr,xx+20*k,yy-7*k,2*k,gl,.31+.12*Math.sin(now*2));
     }
     ambientStats.objects=1;
   }else if(sc.area){
     // Small signature motion near the player's current position; no new colliders.
     if(!worldDraw?.active||scene!==sc){try{worldDraw?.destroy()}catch(_){}worldDraw=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(5.7);scene=sc;}
     worldDraw.clear();const map=sc.area,pl=sc.player;if(!pl)return;
     const nearby=(map.buildings||[]).map(b=>({b,x:(b.door+.5)*TILE,y:(b.y+.3)*TILE}))
       .filter(q=>Math.abs(q.x-pl.x)<430&&Math.abs(q.y-pl.y)<330)
       .sort((a,b)=>(a.x-pl.x)**2+(a.y-pl.y)**2-(b.x-pl.x)**2-(b.y-pl.y)**2).slice(0,opts.quality==='performance'?3:6);
     for(const [i,q] of nearby.entries()){
       const a=(sc.time?.now||0)/1000 + i;
       const c=window.LQVisualV63?.REGIONS?.[map.id]||null;
       if(!c)continue;
       const x=q.x-12,y=q.y-6;
       if(c.kind==='organic'||c.kind==='heritage')line(worldDraw,x,y,x+3*Math.sin(a*.7),y-10,c.a,.30,2);
       else if(c.kind==='industrial')r(worldDraw,x,y,5,3,c.glow,.22+.18*(Math.sin(a*3)+1)/2);
       else if(c.kind==='merchant')r(worldDraw,x,y,7,3,c.b,.32+.12*Math.sin(a));
       else r(worldDraw,x,y,4,4,c.glow,.20+.12*Math.sin(a));
     }
     ambientStats.objects=nearby.length;
   }
 }
 function debug(sc,dt){
   if(!sc||!opts.debug){debugDraw?.clear();return}
   debugMs+=Number.isFinite(dt)?dt:16;if(debugMs<180)return;debugMs=0;
   if(!debugDraw?.active||debugDraw.scene!==sc){try{debugDraw?.destroy()}catch(_){}debugDraw=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(4000)}
   debugDraw.clear();const player=sc.player;if(!player)return;
   if(sc.inside){
     debugDraw.lineStyle(1,0x6ce7ed,.72);
     for(const q of sc.inside.rects||[]){
       if(Math.abs((q.x+q.w/2)-player.x)<360&&Math.abs((q.y+q.h/2)-player.y)<300)
         debugDraw.strokeRect(q.x,q.y,q.w,q.h);
     }
   } else if(sc.area?.__lqPrecise){
     const all=[...sc.area.__lqPrecise.buildings,...sc.area.__lqPrecise.stalls,...sc.area.__lqPrecise.terrain];
     for(const shape of all){
       if(!shape.poly||Math.abs((shape.box.x+shape.box.right)/2-player.x)>340||Math.abs((shape.box.y+shape.box.bottom)/2-player.y)>280)continue;
       debugDraw.lineStyle(1,shape.category==='building'?0xffd06e:shape.category==='stall'?0x5bf6dd:0xff8a94,.70);
       debugDraw.strokePoints(shape.poly,true);
     }
   }
   debugDraw.lineStyle(2,0x51eac6,.75);debugDraw.strokeCircle(player.x,player.y,window.LQPrecisePhysics?.radius||4.5);
 }
 function onEnter(){roomKey='';}
 function onLeave(){
   if(insideDraw?.active){try{insideDraw.destroy()}catch(_){}}
   insideDraw=null;roomKey='';
 }
 function update(sc,dt){drawAmbient(sc,dt);debug(sc,dt)}
 function mount(){if(typeof document==='undefined')return;let old=document.getElementById('lq64-panel');if(old)return old;

   document.head.appendChild(style);
   const div=document.createElement('div');div.id='lq64-panel';div.hidden=true;
   div.innerHTML='<section role="dialog" aria-label="Visual Intelligence V64" aria-modal="true"><h2>V64 · VISUAL INTELLIGENCE</h2><p>Kiểm tra thiết kế theo hình học thực, vùng chiếm chỗ hợp nhất, collider và hoạt cảnh. Lớp đồ họa V64 không thay đổi lối đi hoặc dữ liệu nhiệm vụ.</p><div class="lq64-controls"><button id="lq64-visual">Đồ họa</button><button id="lq64-ambient">Chuyển động</button><button id="lq64-physics">Hiện va chạm</button><select id="lq64-quality" aria-label="Quality"><option value="performance">Performance</option><option value="balanced">Balanced</option><option value="cinematic">Cinematic</option></select><button id="lq64-copy">Sao chép QA</button><button id="lq64-refresh">Phân tích lại</button></div><pre id="lq64-report"></pre><div class="lq64-controls"><button id="lq64-close">Tiếp tục chơi</button></div></section>';
   document.body.appendChild(div);
   function refresh(){const x=report();const pre=div.querySelector('#lq64-report');pre.textContent=`Đã ghi nhận ${x.observedRooms} phòng, trên ${Object.keys(x.byMap).length} map.\nCặp tương tự ≥ 86/100: ${x.totalSimilar}.\nCác vấn đề mật độ/cửa cần xem lại: ${x.issues.length}.\n\nCÁC CẶP CẦN ĐỐI CHIẾU:\n${x.similar.slice(0,15).map(q=>`${q.score}%: ${q.a} ↔ ${q.b}`).join('\n')||'Chưa phát hiện trong các phòng đã mở'}\n\nCẢNH BÁO BỐ CỤC:\n${x.issues.slice(0,15).map(q=>`${q.kind} ${q.coverage}%: ${q.room}`).join('\n')||'Không phát hiện theo tiêu chí kiểm tra'}\n\nChỉ số va chạm: ${JSON.stringify(x.physicsIndex)}\nHiệu ứng gần camera: ${x.render.objects}\n* Những chỉ số này không chứng minh toàn bộ cửa và nhiệm vụ đã được kiểm thử.`;

 function area(s){return String(s?.__lq58?.area||s?.__lq60?.area||'english-town');}
 function roomMode(s){
  if(preferences.preset==='original')return 'original';
  if(preferences.preset==='airy')return 'airy';
  if(preferences.preset==='clean')return 'clean';

 // Keep the primary furniture. Restrict secondary strokes to a few actual object footprints.
 const PRIORITY=/bed|desk|table|counter|workbench|anvil|machine|forge|furnace|clock|organ|piano|throne|display|shelf|cabinet|loom|hearth|stove|herb|plant|books/i;
 const important=new WeakMap();
 function highlights(s){
  let q=important.get(s);if(q && q.items===s.items && q.length===s.items?.length)return q.keys;
  const a=(s.items||[]).filter(it=>PRIORITY.test(String(it.k||'')));
  a.sort((x,y)=>Math.max(1,y.w*y.h)-Math.max(1,x.w*x.h));
  const chosen=[];
  for(const it of a){
    if(chosen.length>=3)break;
    const xx=it.x+it.w*.5, yy=it.y+it.h*.5;
    if(chosen.some(other=>Math.hypot(xx-(other.x+other.w*.5),yy-(other.y+other.h*.5))<2.6))continue;
    chosen.push(it);
  }
  const keys=new Set(chosen);important.set(s,{items:s.items,length:s.items?.length,keys});return keys;
 }
 const stroke=(g,x,y,xx,yy,color,alpha=.3,width=1)=>g.lineStyle(width,color,alpha).lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(xx),Math.round(yy));

 function back(g,s){

 }
 function item(g,it,s){
  if(!highlights(s).has(it))return;

  if(w<28||h<20)return;

 }

 }

            ada: { skin: jr, hair: 2038306, style: `bun`, outfit: 16753920, pants: 4864618, glasses: !0, collar: !0 },
            kai: { skin: Ar, hair: 3811866, style: `short`, outfit: 16744272, pants: 2764618, hat: { kind: `cap`, color: 4861984 } },
            mira: { skin: kr, hair: 12103848, style: `bun`, outfit: 8564863, pants: 3816002, apron: 16777215 },

        function Pr(e) {
          return {
            skin: kr,
            hair: 5913122,
            style: `short`,
            outfit: e,
            pants: 3820154,
            hat: { kind: `cap`, color: Ir(e, 0.75) },
          };
        }
        var Fr = 2826800;
        function Ir(e, t) {
          let n = Math.min(255, Math.round(((e >> 16) & 255) * t)),
            r = Math.min(255, Math.round(((e >> 8) & 255) * t)),
            i = Math.min(255, Math.round((e & 255) * t));
          return (n << 16) | (r << 8) | i;
        }
        function Lr(e, t, n, r, i, a) {
          (e.fillStyle(Fr, 1).fillRect(t - 1, n - 1, r + 2, i + 2),
            e.fillStyle(a, 1).fillRect(t, n, r, i));
        }
        function Rr(e, t, n, r, i) {
          (e.fillStyle(Fr, 1).fillCircle(t, n, r + 1), e.fillStyle(i, 1).fillCircle(t, n, r));
        }
        function zr(e, t, n, r) {
          const __eyeColor = typeof t.eye === 'number' ? t.eye : 2236979;
          // Uniform characters (Gojo) walk in a relaxed way: short steps, arms stay still with hands in the pockets.
          const __k = t.uniform || t.kimono ? 1 : 2;
          let i = n === `side`,
            a = n === `dside` || n === `uside`,
            o = n === `up` || n === `uside`,
            s = r === 1 ? __k : 0,
            c = r === 2 ? __k : 0,
            l = t.uniform || t.kimono ? 0 : r === 1 ? 1 : r === 2 ? -1 : 0,
            u = +!!a,
            d = a ? 0.5 : 0;
          const __glide = !!t.glide;
          // Hovering: a smaller, fainter shadow stays on the ground while everything else is lifted. Every walk frame
          // is identical (it glides, it does not step); the slow up/down bob is done by aura.js on the live sprite.
          if (__glide) {
            e.fillStyle(0, 0.16).fillEllipse(12, 35, 11, 4);
            e.translateCanvas(0, -4);
          } else if (!t.riding) e.fillStyle(0, 0.28).fillEllipse(12, 35, 16, 6);
          // Gliding legs: normal-sized legs, tilted 30 degrees backwards when seen from the side (far leg darker).
          // From the front/back the tilt points along the view direction, so they are drawn as ordinary legs.
          const __glideLegs = () => {
            if (i) {
              const L = 7,
                h = L * Math.cos(Math.PI / 6),
                dx = L * Math.sin(Math.PI / 6);
              const leg = (x, color) => {
                const pt = (u, side) => [x + side - dx * u, 27 + h * u];
                const quad = (u0, u1) => [pt(u0, 0), pt(u0, 5), pt(u1, 5), pt(u1, 0)];
                const fill = (pts, c, stroke) => {
                  e.beginPath();
                  e.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
                  for (let k = 1; k < pts.length; k++) e.lineTo(pts[k][0], pts[k][1]);
                  e.closePath();
                  e.fillStyle(c, 1);
                  e.fillPath();
                  if (stroke) {
                    e.lineStyle(1, Fr, 1);
                    e.strokePath();
                  }
                };
                fill(quad(0, 1), color, true);
                fill(quad(0.72, 1), 3811874, false);
              };
              leg(8, Ir(t.pants, 0.78));
              leg(10, t.pants);
            } else if (a) {
              f(6, 0);
              f(12, 0);
            } else {
              f(6, 0);
              f(13, 0);
            }
          };
          let f = (n, r) => {
            let i = 7 - r;
            (Lr(e, n, 27, 5, i, t.pants), e.fillStyle(3811874, 1).fillRect(n, 27 + i - 2, 5, 2));
          };
          if (
            (__glide
              ? __glideLegs()
              : i
              ? (f(9 + (r === 1 ? -__k : r === 2 ? __k : 0), 0),
                f(9 + (r === 1 ? __k : r === 2 ? -__k : 0), 0))
              : a
                ? (f(6 + (r === 1 ? -1 : +(r === 2)), s),
                  f(12 + (r === 1 ? 1 : r === 2 ? -1 : 0), c))
                : (f(6, s), f(13, c)),
            !i)
          ) {
            let n = a
              ? [
                  [3, 3, l],
                  [17, 4, -l],
                ]
              : [
                  [2, 3, l],
                  [19, 3, -l],
                ];
            for (let [r, i, a] of n)
              (Lr(e, r, 19 + a, i, 8, t.outfit),
                t.uniform || e.fillStyle(t.skin, 1).fillRect(r, 19 + a + 6, i, 2));
          }
          let p = i ? 7 : a ? 6 : 5,
            m = i ? 10 : a ? 12 : 14;
          if (
            (Lr(e, p, 18, m, 10, t.outfit),
            e.fillStyle(Ir(t.outfit, 1.2), 1).fillRect(p + 1, 19, m - 2, 2),
            e.fillStyle(Ir(t.outfit, 0.75), 1).fillRect(p + 1, 26, m - 2, 1),
            t.dress && Lr(e, i ? 6 : a ? 5 : 4, 26, i ? 12 : a ? 14 : 16, 5, Ir(t.outfit, 0.92)),
            !o && !i
              ? (t.collar &&
                  e
                    .fillStyle(16777215, 1)
                    .fillRect(9 + u, 18, 6, 2)
                    .fillTriangle(9 + u, 19, 15 + u, 19, 12 + u, 23),
                t.tie && e.fillStyle(t.tie, 1).fillRect(11 + u, 19, 2, 6),
                t.apron && Lr(e, 7 + u, 21, 10, 7, t.apron))
              : o
                ? t.apron && e.fillStyle(t.apron, 1).fillRect(p, 22, m, 1)
                : t.apron && e.fillStyle(t.apron, 1).fillRect(14, 21, 3, 7),

              (Lr(e, 10, 19 + l, 4, 8, Ir(t.outfit, 0.9)),
              t.uniform || e.fillStyle(t.skin, 1).fillRect(10, 19 + l + 6, 4, 2)),

              (o
                ? Lr(e, 4, 10, a ? 15 : 16, 12, t.hair)
                : i
                  ? Lr(e, 4, 10, 6, 11, t.hair)
                  : (Lr(e, 3, 10, 5, 11, t.hair), Lr(e, a ? 17 : 16, 10, 5, 11, t.hair))),
            // Kimono wearers hold their head 1px higher (proud posture); undone after the head/hat are drawn.
            t.kimono && e.translateCanvas(0, -1),
            Rr(e, 12 + d, 12, 8.5, t.skin),
            t.style !== `bald` &&
              !((t.style === `storm` || t.style === `ryomen` || t.style === `urchin`) && !t.hat) &&
              Rr(e, 12 + d, 11, 8.5, t.hair),
            n === `uside` && e.fillStyle(t.skin, 1).fillEllipse(19, 14.5, 3, 7),
            !o)
          ) {
            let n = i || a ? 14.5 : 12,
              r = i ? 11 : a ? 12 : 14;
            e.fillStyle(t.skin, 1).fillEllipse(n, 14.5, r, 11);
            let o = i ? [17] : a ? [11, 16] : [8, 14];
            for (let t of o)
              (e.fillStyle(__eyeColor, 1).fillRect(t, 13, 2, 3),
                e.fillStyle(16777215, 1).fillRect(t, 13, 1, 1));
            (e.fillStyle(16747146, 0.5),
              i
                ? e.fillRect(15, 16, 2, 1)
                : a
                  ? e.fillRect(9, 16, 2, 1).fillRect(18, 16, 2, 1)
                  : e.fillRect(6, 16, 2, 1).fillRect(16, 16, 2, 1),
              t.beard === void 0
                ? e.fillStyle(10506826, 1).fillRect(i ? 18 : a ? 14 : 11, 18, 2, 1)
                : (e.fillStyle(t.beard, 1),
                  i
                    ? e.fillRect(14, 17, 6, 4)
                    : a
                      ? e.fillRect(9, 17, 9, 4)
                      : e.fillRect(7, 17, 10, 4)),
              t.glasses &&
                (e.lineStyle(1, 2236979, 1),
                i
                  ? e.strokeRect(15.5, 12.5, 4.5, 4)
                  : a
                    ? e
                        .strokeRect(10, 12.5, 4, 4)
                        .strokeRect(15, 12.5, 4, 4)
                        .lineBetween(14, 14.5, 15, 14.5)
                    : e
                        .strokeRect(7, 12.5, 4.5, 4)
                        .strokeRect(12.5, 12.5, 4.5, 4)
                        .lineBetween(11.5, 14.5, 12.5, 14.5)));
          }
          t.style === `bun` && Rr(e, i ? 7 : 12 + d, i ? 6 : 3.5, 3.5, t.hair);
          let h = t.hat;
          if (h) {
            let t = h.color;
            if (h.kind === `chef`) {
              for (let [n, r, i] of [
                [8, 4, 3.5],
                [12, 3, 4],
                [16, 4, 3.5],
              ])
                Rr(e, n + d, r, i, t);
              Lr(e, 6 + d, 5, 12, 5, t);
            } else
              h.kind === `helmet`
                ? (Lr(e, 3 + d, 2, 18, 8, t),
                  e.fillStyle(Ir(t, 1.2), 1).fillRect(5 + d, 3, 14, 2),
                  Lr(e, 2, 9, i ? 22 : a ? 21 : 20, 2, Ir(t, 0.85)),

                : (Lr(e, 4 + d, 3, 16, o ? 7 : 6, t),
                  e.fillStyle(Ir(t, 1.25), 1).fillRect(6 + d, 4, 12, 1),
                  o || Lr(e, i ? 14 : a ? 5 : 3, 8, i ? 9 : a ? 17 : 18, 2, Ir(t, 0.8)),
                  h.kind === `police` &&
                    !o &&
                    !i &&
                    e.fillStyle(15909424, 1).fillRect(11 + (a ? 2 : 0), 5, 3, 3));
          }
          if (t.kimono) e.translateCanvas(0, 1);
        }
        // ---- Anime-inspired hairstyles. The sprite is 24x38 and the head top sits near y=2, so volume comes from
        // overlapping puffs, side spikes, jagged tufts and loose strands instead of tall spikes.
        // Each view lists: blocks (outlined rectangles, always drawn), puffs/knots (outlined circles),
        // tris (flat triangles: x1,y1,x2,y2,x3,y3) and lines (strand highlights). Puffs, knots, tris and lines
        // are hidden when a hat is worn.
        const __top = (ks, h = 4, tip = 0) =>
          ks.map((k, i) => [12 + k - 2, h, 12 + k + 2, h, 12 + k + (i % 2 ? -1.5 : 1.5), tip]);
        const __backBase = { puffs: [[12, 10, 9.2]], tris: __top([-6, -2, 2, 6]) };
        // Mirror the left half of a polygon (flat [x,y,...] running from the top-centre point to the bottom-centre point)
        // onto the right half around x=12, so front/back views come out perfectly symmetric.
        const __sym = (h) => {
          const out = h.slice();
          for (let i = h.length - 4; i >= 2; i -= 2) out.push(24 - h[i], h[i + 1]);
          return out;
        };

        function __lqDrawHair(g, c, dir, dx) {
          const spec = __hairSpecs[c.style];
          if (!spec) return;
          const hair = typeof c.hair === 'number' ? c.hair : 0x5a3b2a;
          const view = dir === 'up' || dir === 'uside' ? spec.back : dir === 'side' ? spec.side : spec.front;
          if (!view) return;
          for (const [x, y, w, h] of view.blocks || []) Lr(g, x + dx, y, w, h, hair);
          if (c.hat) return;
          for (const p of view.polys || []) {
            // One filled silhouette with a thin dark outline.
            g.beginPath();
            g.moveTo(p[0] + dx, p[1]);
            for (let i = 2; i < p.length; i += 2) g.lineTo(p[i] + dx, p[i + 1]);
            g.closePath();
            g.fillStyle(hair, 1);
            g.fillPath();
            g.lineStyle(1, Fr, 1);
            g.strokePath();
          }
          const blobs = [...(view.puffs || []), ...(view.knots || [])];
          if (view.outline === 'clean') {
            // All outlines first, then all fills, so overlapping shapes merge into one silhouette without inner seams.
            g.fillStyle(Fr, 1);
            for (const [x, y, r] of blobs) g.fillCircle(x + dx, y, r + 1);
            g.lineStyle(2, Fr, 1);
            for (const q of view.tris || []) g.strokeTriangle(q[0] + dx, q[1], q[2] + dx, q[3], q[4] + dx, q[5]);
            g.fillStyle(hair, 1);
            for (const [x, y, r] of blobs) g.fillCircle(x + dx, y, r);
          } else for (const [x, y, r] of blobs) Rr(g, x + dx, y, r, hair);
          g.fillStyle(hair, 1);
          for (const q of view.tris || []) g.fillTriangle(q[0] + dx, q[1], q[2] + dx, q[3], q[4] + dx, q[5]);
          if (view.outline === true) {
            g.lineStyle(1, Fr, 1);
            for (const q of view.tris || []) g.strokeTriangle(q[0] + dx, q[1], q[2] + dx, q[3], q[4] + dx, q[5]);
          }
          const lum = (((hair >> 16) & 255) + ((hair >> 8) & 255) + (hair & 255)) / 3;
          g.lineStyle(1, Ir(hair, lum < 90 ? 1.9 : 0.8), 1);
          for (const l of view.lines || []) g.lineBetween(l[0] + dx, l[1], l[2] + dx, l[3]);
        }        const __v11BaseCharacter = zr;
        zr = function (g, c, dir, frame) {
          __v11BaseCharacter(g, c, dir, frame);
          // Riding a shadow beast (Megumi): eyes shut, relaxed. The open eyes are painted over with skin, then a thin
          // dark line is drawn where each eye was.
          if (c.riding && dir !== 'up' && dir !== 'uside') {
            const exs = dir === 'side' ? [17] : dir === 'dside' ? [11, 16] : [8, 14];
            for (const ex of exs) {
              g.fillStyle(c.skin, 1).fillRect(ex, 13, 2, 3);
              g.fillStyle(0x241c1e, 1).fillRect(ex, 15, 2, 1);
            }
          }
          // A few one-pixel material highlights, carried in cached textures only.
          const col = typeof c.outfit === 'number' ? c.outfit : 0x5a84bb;
          const bright = (v, k) => Math.min(255, Math.round(v * k));
          const hi =
            (bright((col >> 16) & 255, 1.32) << 16) |
            (bright((col >> 8) & 255, 1.32) << 8) |
            bright(col & 255, 1.32);
          const face = dir === 'down' || dir === 'dside';
          g.fillStyle(hi, 0.42).fillRect(8, 20, 7, 1);
          g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.16).fillRect(10, 23, 2, 1);
          g.fillStyle(0x141b2d, 0.34).fillRect(7, 33, 4, 1).fillRect(14, 33, 4, 1);
          if (face) {
            g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.27).fillRect(7, 11, 2, 1);
            g.fillRect(15, 11, 2, 1);
          }
          if (c.apron) {
            g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.2).fillRect(9, 23, 5, 1);
          }
          if (c.glasses) {
            g.fillStyle(0xd7f1ff, 0.19).fillRect(8, 13, 2, 1);
          }

          const isSide = dir === 'side',
            isDiag = dir === 'dside' || dir === 'uside',
            isBack = dir === 'up' || dir === 'uside',
            dx = isDiag ? 0.5 : 0;
          if (c.style === 'bob') {
            if (isBack) Lr(g, 4, 10, isDiag ? 15 : 16, 10, hairColor);
            else if (isSide) Lr(g, 4, 10, 6, 9, hairColor);

              Lr(g, 2, 10, 4, 9, hairColor);
              Lr(g, 18, 10, 4, 9, hairColor);
            }

            else if (isSide) Lr(g, 4, 11, 3, 10, hairColor);
            else Lr(g, 19, 12, 3, 8, hairColor);

              [19, 7],

          if (c.eyeGlow && typeof c.eye === 'number' && dir !== 'up' && dir !== 'uside') {

        var Br = [`down`, `up`, `side`, `dside`, `uside`];
        function Vr(e, t, n, costume) {
          for (let r of Br)
            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
              let a = `ch_${t}_${r}_${i}`;
              if (e.textures.exists(a)) continue;
              let o = e.add.graphics();
              (zr(o, n, r, i),
                costume?.(o, r, i),
                o.generateTexture(a, 24, 38),
                o.destroy(),
                e.textures.get(a).setFilter(Y.Textures.FilterMode.NEAREST));
            }
        }
        // Custom looks get 12 extra pixels above the head so tall spikes are not cut off by the 38px sprite.
        const __LQ_PAD = 12;
        function VrTall(e, t, n, pad) {
          for (let r of Br)
            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
              let a = `ch_${t}_${r}_${i}`;
              if (e.textures.exists(a)) continue;
              let o = e.add.graphics();
              (o.translateCanvas(0, pad),
                zr(o, n, r, i),
                o.generateTexture(a, 24, 38 + pad),
                o.destroy(),
                e.textures.get(a).setFilter(Y.Textures.FilterMode.NEAREST));
            }
        }        // ---- Character creator support: turn the player's saved "look" into a sprite description ----
        // Hairstyles were renamed; looks saved under the old names keep working.
        const __lqStyleAlias = { gojo: 'storm', itadori: 'scruffy', megumi: 'thorn', nobara: 'flare', maki: 'highpony', geto: 'longbun', nanami: 'swept', sukuna: 'blaze', yuta: 'tousled', toji: 'crop' };
        const __lqStyle = (s) => __lqStyleAlias[s] || s || 'short';
        function __lqLookKey(l) {
          const s = JSON.stringify([
            l.skin,
            l.hair,
            __lqStyle(l.style),
            l.eye,
            l.outfit,
            l.pants,
            l.hat && [l.hat.kind, l.hat.color],
            !!l.glasses,
            !!l.dress,
            l.backpack || 0,
            !!l.blindfold,
            !!l.collar,
            l.tie || 0,
            !!l.uniform,
            !!l.eyeGlow,
            l.aura === 'infinity',
            !!l.extraEyes,
            !!l.faceMarks,
            !!l.kimono,
            l.trim || 0,
            !!l.riding,
          ]);
          let h = 5381;
          for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = ((h << 5) + h + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
          return 'pl' + h.toString(36);
        }
        function __lqLookToAppearance(l) {
          return {
            skin: l.skin,
            hair: l.hair,
            style: __lqStyle(l.style),
            eye: l.eye,
            outfit: l.outfit,
            pants: l.pants,
            hat: l.hat ? { kind: l.hat.kind, color: l.hat.color } : undefined,
            glasses: !!l.glasses,
            dress: !!l.dress,
            backpack: l.backpack || undefined,
            blindfold: !!l.blindfold,
            collar: !!l.collar,
            tie: l.tie || undefined,
            uniform: !!l.uniform,
            eyeGlow: !!l.eyeGlow,
            // Infinity users glide: they hover above the ground with their legs trailing behind.
            glide: l.aura === 'infinity',
            extraEyes: !!l.extraEyes,
            faceMarks: !!l.faceMarks,
            kimono: !!l.kimono,
            trim: l.trim || undefined,
            riding: !!l.riding,
          };
        }
        window.LQCharacter = {
          pad: __LQ_PAD,
          size: { w: 24, h: 38 + __LQ_PAD },
          keyFor: __lqLookKey,
          appearance: __lqLookToAppearance,
          ensure(scene, look) {
            const key = __lqLookKey(look);
            VrTall(scene, key, __lqLookToAppearance(look), __LQ_PAD);
            return key;
          },
          imageFor(scene, look, dir = 'down', frame = 0) {
            const key = this.ensure(scene, look);
            return scene.textures.get(`ch_${key}_${dir}_${frame}`).getSourceImage();
          },
        };
        function Hr(e, t, n) {
          let r = Math.abs(t),
            i = Math.abs(n);
          (r < 0.01 && i < 0.01) ||
            (i < r * 0.4
              ? ((e.dir = `side`), (e.flip = t < 0))
              : r < i * 0.4
                ? (e.dir = n < 0 ? `up` : `down`)
                : ((e.dir = n < 0 ? `uside` : `dside`), (e.flip = t < 0)));
        }
        function Ur(e, t, n, r) {
          // Sprites flagged lqSlow (kimono wearers) change walk frame 2.4x less often: a slow, dignified pace.
          let i = r ? 1 + (Math.floor(n.phase / (e.lqSlow ? 24 : 10)) % 2) : 0,
            a = `ch_${t}_${n.dir}_${i}`;
          (e.texture.key !== a && e.setTexture(a),
            e.setFlipX(n.flip && n.dir !== `down` && n.dir !== `up`));
        }

            create() {
              this.store.load();
              // Free exploration build: Art District is available on every save (old or new).
              // Only the map access flag changes; NPC missions and achievements are untouched.
              if (!this.store.game.artDistrictUnlocked) {
                this.store.game.artDistrictUnlocked = true;
                this.store.save();

            checkArtDistrictAccess() {
              const game = this.store.game;
              if (game.artDistrictUnlocked || this.artDistrictPreview) return !0;

              // These are the original progression quotas. The wandering artists do not count as town missions.
              const countDone = (areaId, excluded = []) =>
                Te(we(areaId)).filter((npc) => !excluded.includes(npc.id) && this.store.get(npc.id).goalDone).length;
              const englishDone = countDone(`english-town`, [`rook`]);
              const mineralsDone = countDone(`mining-town`, [`vex`, `morrow`]);
              const historyDone = this.artHistoryDone();
              const guideIds = [`rook`, `vex`, `morrow`];
              const marksDone = guideIds.filter((id) => this.store.get(id).goalDone).length;
              const unlocked = englishDone >= 4 && mineralsDone >= 3 && marksDone === 3 && historyDone;

              if (unlocked) {
                game.artDistrictUnlocked = !0;
                this.store.save();
                this.quests?.toast([
                  `🎨 THE THREE MARKS ARE COMPLETE`,
                  `Rook, Vex and Morrow have revealed the forgotten freight route.`,
                  `The hidden tunnel in Minerals Town is now open.`,
                ]);

                  ? this.area.warps.find((item) => item.requiresArtDistrict)
                  : null;
                if (warp && this.add && this.areaObjs) {
                  const label = this.add.text((warp.x + warp.w / 2) * 32, warp.y * 32 - 8, warp.label, {

                  }).setOrigin(0.5, 1).setDepth(12);
                  this.areaObjs.push(label);
                }
              }
              return unlocked;

            placeLabel(e) {

                  e.label.setColor(`#ffd36b`);

            buildArea() {

            unstick() {
              if (this.inside || this._lqSurface || !this.blocked(this.player.x, this.player.y)) return;
              let e = Math.floor(this.player.x / 32),
                t = Math.floor(this.player.y / 32);
              for (let n = 1; n < 10; n++)
                for (let r = -n; r <= n; r++)
                  for (let i = -n; i <= n; i++) {
                    if (Math.max(Math.abs(i), Math.abs(r)) !== n) continue;
                    let a = ce(e + i, t + r);
                    if (!this.blocked(a.x, a.y)) {
                      this.player.setPosition(a.x, a.y);
                      return;
                    }
                  }
            }
            fitCamera() {

              this.cameras.main.setBounds(Math.min(0, (n - i) / 2), Math.min(0, (r - a) / 2), i, a);
            }

            drawWorld() {
              let e = `world_${this.area.id}`;
              if (!this.textures.exists(e)) {
