            switchArea(e, t, n) {
              if (window.LQCareerWorlds && !window.LQCareerWorlds.allowed(e)){window.LQCareerWorlds.feedback(this,e);return;}
              if (this.switching) return;
              if(this.area===lqValley && !window.LQValleyPlay.flushTrail(this))return;
              cancelTownAutopilot();
              this.switching = !0;
              let r = this.cameras.main;
              const valleyTravel = this.area === lqValley || e === lqValley.id;
              let fallback;
              let waiting = true;
              const complete = () => {
                  if (!waiting) return;
                  waiting = false;
                  clearTimeout(fallback);
                  r.off(`camerafadeoutcomplete`, complete);
                  if (!this.player?.active) return;
                  let i = this.inside;
                  if (i) {
                    LQ57.onLeave();
              LQ58.onLeave(); LQ59.onLeave(); LQ60.onLeave(); LQ61.onLeave(); LQ62.onLeave(); LQ63.onLeave(); LQ64.onLeave();
                    for (let e of i.objs) e.destroy();
                    this.inside = null;
                  }
                  (this.clearArea(), (this.area = we(e)), (this.store.game.area = this.area.id));
                  if (this.store.game.rpg) delete this.store.game.rpg.lastInterior;
                  let a = !this.store.game.visited.includes(this.area.id);
                  (a && this.store.game.visited.push(this.area.id),
                    this.quests.setArea(this.area),
                    this.buildArea());
                  LQ57.decorateExterior(this);
                  let o = ce(t.x, t.y);
                  (this.player.setScale(1).setPosition(o.x, o.y),

                    window.LQValleyPlay.recordArrival(this),
                    this.store.save(),

                    this.quests.toast([
                      `📍 ${this.area.name}`,
                      n,

                    ]),
                    (this.switching = !1));
              };
              r.once(`camerafadeoutcomplete`, complete);
              r.fadeOut(500, 0, 0, 0);
              if (valleyTravel) fallback = setTimeout(() => {
                if (waiting) r.fadeEffect.reset();
                complete();
              }, 1000);
            }
            setHidden(e, t) {
              (e.sprite.setVisible(!t), e.label.setVisible(!t));
            }
            blockedInside(e, t) {
              let n = this.inside,
                r = n.k,
                i = 32 * r,
                a = 7 * r;
              return (
                e < Yr + 6 * r + a ||
                e > Yr + n.spec.w * i - 6 * r - a ||
                t < Xr + 2 * i + a ||
                t > Xr + n.spec.h * i - 4 * r - a ||
                n.rects.some(
                  (n) => e > n.x - a && e < n.x + n.w + a && t > n.y - a && t < n.y + n.h + a,
                )
              );
            }
            blocked(e, t) {
              if (this.inside) return this.blockedInside(e, t);
              if(this.area?.__lqPrecise)return LQPrecisePhysics.playerBlocked(this.area,e,t);
              // Defensive fallback for a map without precision geometry.
              return !Number.isFinite(e)||!Number.isFinite(t)||e<8||t<8||
                e>this.area.w*32-8||t>this.area.h*32-8||
                this.walls.some(n=>e>n.x-7&&e<n.x+n.width+7&&t>n.y-7&&t<n.y+n.height+7);
            }
            nearestNpc(e = 48) {
              let t = null,
                n = e;
              for (let e of this.npcs) {
                if (!e.sprite.visible) continue;
                let r = Y.Math.Distance.Between(
                  this.player.x,
                  this.player.y,
                  e.sprite.x,
                  e.sprite.y,
                );
                r < n && ((n = r), (t = e));
              }
              return t;
            }
            tryTalk() {
              if (window.LQValleyPlay.interact(this)) return;
              if (
                this.chat.isOpen ||
                this.journal.isOpen ||
                this.settings.isOpen ||
                this.quests.isOpen ||
                this.helper.isOpen ||
                this.briefing.isOpen ||
                this.sleeping ||
                this.switching
              )
                return;
              let e = this.nearestNpc(this.inside ? 56 * this.inside.k : 48);
              if (!this.inside && this.doorCooldown <= 0 && this.doorAhead()) {
                this.enterBuilding(this.doorAhead());
                return;
              }
              if (e)
                (Hr(e.face, this.player.x - e.sprite.x, this.player.y - e.sprite.y),
                  Hr(this.pFace, e.sprite.x - this.player.x, e.sprite.y - this.player.y),
                  this.chat.open(e.def));
              else if (this.bedNear()) this.trySleep();
              else if (this.useStairs()) return;
              else {
                let gate = this.portalNear();
                if (gate) this.useGate(gate);
                else if (!this.inside && window.LQ52?.tryInteract?.(this)) return;
                else this.inspectObject();
              }
            }
            gateKeeper(e) {
              return e.guard
                ? (this.npcs.find((t) => t.def.id === e.guard && t.inside) ?? null)
                : null;
            }
            gateOpen(e) {
              return !e.guard || this.gateKeeper(e) !== null;
            }
            useGate(e) {
              let t = this.store.game;

              });
            }

              const rects = t.items
                .filter(gr)
                .map((item) => ({

                  y: Xr + item.y * unit,
                  w: item.w * unit,
                  h: item.h * unit,
                }));
              this.inside = { b: e, spec: t, k, rects, objs, out, floor: chosen };
              LQ57.onEnter(this);
              LQ58.onEnter(this);
              LQ59.onEnter(this); LQ60.onEnter(this); LQ61.onEnter(this); LQ62.onEnter(this); LQ63.onEnter(this); LQ64.onEnter(this);
              window.LQCareerWorlds?.onInteriorEnter?.(this);
              if (!chosen) {
                let guest = 0;
                for (const npc of this.npcs) {
                  const place = this.area.places[npc.slot.place];
                  if (
                    vr[npc.slot.place] !== e.name ||
                    npc.slot.kind === 'sleep' ||
                    !npc.sprite.visible ||
                    !place

                    continue;
                  const pt = t.spots[npc.def.id] ||

                }
              }
              this.player.setScale(k).setPosition(targetX, targetY);
              // Avoid spawning in furniture or beyond the interior collision margin.
              if (this.blocked(targetX, targetY)) {
                for (let y = t.h - 2; y >= 3; y--) {
                  let found = false;
                  for (let dx = 0; dx < t.w - 2; dx++)
                    for (const x of [Math.floor(t.w / 2) + dx, Math.floor(t.w / 2) - dx]) {
                      if (x < 1 || x >= t.w - 1) continue;
                      const px = Yr + (x + 0.5) * unit,
                        py = Xr + (y + 0.5) * unit;
                      if (!this.blocked(px, py)) {
                        this.player.setPosition(px, py);
                        found = true;
                        break;

                  if (found) break;
                }
              }

            }
            nearStairs() {
              if (!this.inside) return null;
              const stair = this.inside.spec.items.find((v) => v.k === 'stairs');
              if (!stair) return null;
              const size = 32 * this.inside.k;
              const x = Yr + (stair.x + stair.w / 2) * size,
                y = Xr + (stair.y + stair.h / 2) * size;
              return Y.Math.Distance.Between(this.player.x, this.player.y, x, y) < size * 2.25
                ? stair
                : null;
            }
            useStairs() {
              const inside = this.inside;
              if (!inside || !this.nearStairs()) return false;
              if (!_r[inside.b.name + '::2']) return false;
              this.enterBuilding(inside.b, inside.floor === 0 ? 1 : 0, { out: inside.out });
              return true;
            }
            mysteryInspect(item) {
              if (!this.inside || !item) return false;
              const game = this.store.game || (this.store.game = {});
              if (!game.rpg || typeof game.rpg !== `object`) game.rpg = {};
              const rpg = game.rpg;
              if (
                !rpg.inventory ||
                typeof rpg.inventory !== `object` ||
                Array.isArray(rpg.inventory)
              )
                rpg.inventory = { note: 1 };
              if (!rpg.mystery || typeof rpg.mystery !== `object` || Array.isArray(rpg.mystery))
                rpg.mystery = {};
              const m = rpg.mystery;
              const addItem = (id, count = 1) =>
                (rpg.inventory[id] = Math.max(0, (Number(rpg.inventory[id]) || 0) + count));
              const award = (key, title, body, money = 0, rep = 0, itemId = ``, itemName = ``) => {
                if (m[key]) {
                  this.store.save();
                  return true;
                }
                m[key] = 1;
                if (itemId) addItem(itemId, 1);
                if (money || rep) this.gain(money, rep);
                this.store.save();
                const rewards = [];
                if (itemName) rewards.push(`Received item: ${itemName}`);
                if (money) rewards.push(`+$${money}`);
                if (rep) rewards.push(`+${rep} reputation`);
                this.quests.toast([
                  `🗝️ ${title}`,
                  body + (rewards.length ? ` · ` + rewards.join(' · ') : ``),
                ]);
                return true;
              };
              if (
                this.inside.b.name === `Library` &&
                this.inside.floor === 1 &&
                [`board`, `desk`, `shelf`].includes(item.k)
              ) {
                if (m.archiveNote) {
                  this.quests.toast([
                    `📚 The Secret Archive is already cleared.`,
                    `You keep the old note for the next clue.`,
                  ]);
                  return true;
                }
                return award(
                  `archiveNote`,
                  `Mystery Quest: Forgotten Archive`,
                  `You found an old archive note pointing to a hidden attic workshop.`,
                  35,
                  2,
                  `archive_note`,
                  `Old Archive Note`,
                );
              }
              if (
                this.inside.b.name === `Hero's Home` &&
                this.inside.floor === 1 &&
                [`crate`, `desk`, `board`].includes(item.k)
              ) {
                if (!m.archiveNote) {
                  this.quests.toast([
                    `🔐 Something is hidden here.`,
                    `Search the Secret Archive in the Library first.`,
                  ]);
                  return true;
                }
                if (m.atticKey) {
                  this.quests.toast([
                    `🧰 This attic has already been searched.`,
                    `The rusty key and map fragment are in your inventory.`,
                  ]);
                  return true;
                }
                return award(
                  `atticKey`,
                  `Mystery Quest: Attic Workshop`,
                  `Behind the crates you discover a rusty mine key and a torn map fragment.`,
                  55,
                  3,
                  `rusty_key`,
                  `Rusty Mine Key`,
                );
              }
              if (
                this.inside.b.name === `Great Mine` &&
                this.inside.floor === 1 &&
                item.k === `crystal`
              ) {
                if (!m.atticKey) {
                  this.quests.toast([
                    `⛏️ The crystal wall hums softly.`,
                    `A hidden key from another mysterious place may be important.`,
                  ]);
                  return true;
                }
                if (m.crystalHeart) {
                  this.quests.toast([
                    `💎 The Crystal Chamber has already been solved.`,
                    `The Crystal Heart is safe in your inventory.`,
                  ]);
                  return true;
                }
                return award(
                  `crystalHeart`,
                  `Mystery Quest: Crystal Chamber`,
                  `The chamber reacts to the rusty key. You uncover the Crystal Heart and finish the mystery trail.`,
                  90,
                  5,
                  `crystal_heart`,
                  `Crystal Heart`,
                );
              }
              return false;
            }
            inspectObject() {
              if (!this.inside) return false;
              if (LQ58.inspect(this)) return true;
              const size = 32 * this.inside.k;
              let nearest = null,
                dist = Infinity;
              for (const item of this.inside.spec.items) {
                if (!__lqV7ObjectHints[item.k]) continue;
                const x = Yr + (item.x + item.w / 2) * size,
                  y = Xr + (item.y + item.h / 2) * size;
                const d = Y.Math.Distance.Between(this.player.x, this.player.y, x, y);
                if (d < dist) {
                  dist = d;
                  nearest = item;
                }
              }
              if (!nearest || dist > size * 2.25) return false;
              try {
                const inspection = {
                  building: this.inside.b.name,
                  object: nearest.k,
                  floor: this.inside.floor || 0,
                  area: this.area.id,
                };
                window.LQV8?.onEvent('inspect', inspection);
                window.LQStory?.onEvent?.('inspect', inspection);
              } catch (_) {}
              if (this.mysteryInspect(nearest)) return true;
              if (window.LQ52?.interiorInteract?.(this, nearest)) return true;
              if (LQ57.inspect(this)) return true;
              const msg = __lqV7ObjectHints[nearest.k];
              if (msg)
                this.quests.toast(['🔎 ' + msg, 'Practice: describe this object in English!']);
              return !!msg;
            }

            tickClock() {
              if (this.area === lqValley) return;
              let e = this.store.game;
              ((e.minutes += Zr(e.minutes) ? qr : Kr),
                !this.sleeping &&
                  (e.minutes >= 1440 && ((e.minutes -= 1440), this.newDay()),
                  this.updateSchedules(),
                  ++this.ticks % 5 == 0 && this.store.save()));
            }
            newDay() {
              let e = this.store.game;
              (e.day++, (e.money += 10 + e.rep * 3));
              for (let t of Object.keys(e.quests)) t.startsWith(`fav-`) && delete e.quests[t];
              this.quests.toast([`☀ A new day! Press Q to see today's favors.`]);
            }
            bedNear() {
              let e = this.inside;
              // Chapter 2 (js/past.js): the player also sleeps in the Brown Family Home in the village of 70 years ago.
              if (
                !e ||
                (e.b.name !== `Hero's Home` &&
                  !(window.LQPast && window.LQPast.canSleepIn && window.LQPast.canSleepIn(e.b.name)))
              )
                return !1;
              let t = e.spec.items.find((e) => e.k === `bed`);
              if (!t) return !1;
              let n = 32 * e.k,
                r = Yr + (t.x + t.w / 2) * n,
                i = Xr + (t.y + t.h / 2) * n;
              return Y.Math.Distance.Between(this.player.x, this.player.y, r, i) < 2.3 * n;
            }
            trySleep() {
              // Chapter 2 days are counted by js/past.js (it handles the whole night when it returns true).
              if (window.LQPast && window.LQPast.sleep && window.LQPast.sleep(this)) return;
              let e = this.store.game;
              if (!Zr(e.minutes)) {
                this.quests.toast([`😴 You are not sleepy yet.`, `Come back to bed after 19:00.`]);
                return;
              }
              ((this.sleeping = !0), this.store.save());
              let t = this.cameras.main;
              (t.fadeOut(900, 0, 0, 0),
                t.once(`camerafadeoutcomplete`, () => {
                  let n = e.minutes >= 1140;
                  ((e.minutes = 420),
                    n && this.newDay(),
                    this.snapNpcs(),
                    this.store.save(),
                    t.fadeIn(900, 0, 0, 0),
                    this.quests.toast([
                      `💤 You slept well. Day ${e.day}, ${se(e.minutes)}.`,
                      `The town is waking up!`,
                    ]),
                    (this.sleeping = !1));
                }));
            }
            snapNpcs() {
              for (let e of this.npcs) {
                if (e.inside) continue;
                e.slot = ae(e.def.id, this.store.game.minutes);
                let t = ce(this.area.places[e.slot.place].x, this.area.places[e.slot.place].y);
                (e.sprite.setPosition(t.x, t.y),
                  this.placeLabel(e),
                  (e.path = []),
                  this.setHidden(e, e.slot.kind === `sleep`));
              }
            }
            updateSchedules() {
              for (let e of this.npcs) {
                let t = ae(e.def.id, this.store.game.minutes);
                if (t === e.slot) continue;
                if (e.inside) {
                  let t = this.inside;
                  ((e.inside = !1),
                    e.sprite.setScale(1).setPosition(t.out.x, t.out.y),
